  24:     // RPC dönüşü (text) gövdede gelir; 204 gelirse _request null döner -> 'ok'.
  25:     const result = await this._request('POST', 'rpc/upsert_wa_groups', {
  26:       body: { p_agent_id: agentId, p_rows: body },
  27:     });
  28:     return result === 'not_connected' ? 'not_connected' : 'ok';
  29:   }
  30: 
  31:   async fetchSelection(agentId) {
  32:     const rows = await this._request('GET', `wa_group_selection?select=group_jid&${this._agentFilter(agentId)}`);
  33:     return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).map((r) => r.group_jid);
  34:   }
  35: }
  36: 
  37: function parseMaxSessions(raw) {
  38:   const n = Number.parseInt(raw || '', 10);
  39:   return Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0 ? n : DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS;
  40: }
  41: 
  42: // Baileys soketi: mesaj geçmişi ve çevrimiçi işaretleme kapalı; messages.upsert dinleyicisi
  43: // bu fabrikada veya yöneticide EKLENMEZ (AC-4).
  44: function makeSocket(opts) {
  45:   return makeWASocket({
  46:     auth: opts.auth,
  47:     logger: pino({ level: 'silent' }),
  48:     printQRInTerminal: false,
  49:     syncFullHistory: false,
  50:     markOnlineOnConnect: false,
  51:     shouldSyncHistoryMessage: () => false,
  52:   });
  53: }
  54: 
  55: function startSessions(env = process.env) {
  56:   const url = env.SUPABASE_URL;
  57:   const serviceKey = env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  58:   if (!url || !serviceKey) {
  59:     console.error('[wa] SUPABASE_URL/SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY yok; kişisel oturumlar başlatılmadı');
  60:     return null;
  61:   }
  62: 
  63:   const webhookSecret = env.BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET;
  64:   if (!webhookSecret) {
  65:     console.error('[wa] BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET yok; seçili grup ilanları iletilmez');
  66:   }
  67: 
  68:   const manager = new SessionManager({
  69:     rest: new SessionsRest({ url, serviceKey }),
  70:     makeSocket,
  71:     authRoot: env.WA_AUTH_ROOT || path.join(__dirname, '..', 'wa-auth'),
  72:     key: env.WA_AUTH_KEY,
  73:     maxSessions: parseMaxSessions(env.WA_MAX_SESSIONS),
  74:     webhook: { url: env.WEBHOOK_URL || DEFAULT_WEBHOOK_URL, secret: webhookSecret },
  75:   });
  76: 
  77:   manager.start().catch((e) => {
  78:     console.error('[wa] oturum yöneticisi başlatılamadı', e && (e.name || 'error'));
  79:   });
  80:   return manager;
  81: }
  82: 
  83: module.exports = { startSessions, makeSocket };
===== scraper/orchestrator.py =====
   1: # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
   2: """Scraper worker giriş noktası.
   3: 
   4: Baileys sidecar'ın POST ettiği /baileys-webhook mesajlarını kuyruğa alır,
   5: sabit boyutlu bir thread pool ile paralel işler: parse (Gemini) -> quality
   6: gate -> fingerprint dedup -> raw_listings'e yaz.
   7: 
   8: Mimari, maviLojistik/src/parsers/veri_cekici_ayristirici.py'deki
   9: queue.Queue + ThreadPoolExecutor + üç katmanlı dedup desenine karşılık gelir.
  10: """
  11: 
  12: import asyncio
  13: import hmac
  14: import json
  15: import logging
  16: import os
  17: import sys
  18: import threading
  19: from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
  20: from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
  21: from queue import Queue
  22: from typing import Optional, Set
  23: 
  24: from api_key_manager import get_default_manager
  25: from fingerprint import compute_fingerprint
  26: from parser import parse_listing_message
  27: from quality_gate import is_safe_to_auto_approve, score_listing
  28: from supabase_client import (
  29:     bump_sidecar_counter,
  30:     fingerprint_exists,
  31:     get_client,
  32:     get_threshold,
  33:     ingest_personal_listing,
  34:     insert_raw_listing,
  35:     is_group_selected,
  36: )
  37: 
  38: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s")
  39: logger = logging.getLogger("scraper.orchestrator")
  40: 
  41: MAX_WORKERS = 10
  42: 
  43: message_queue: "Queue[dict]" = Queue()
  44: # Aynı mesaj ID'sinin kuyrukta iki kez işlenmesini önleyen bellek-içi set
  45: # (DB'deki unique index son savunma hattı, bu set ise anlık race-condition koruması).
  46: _active_ids_lock = threading.Lock()
  47: _active_ids: Set[str] = set()
  48: 
  49: 
  50: def _personal_outcome_key(status: str, score: Optional[float]) -> str:
  51:     """Kişisel yazım sonucunu sayaç anahtarına çevirir (Davranış Sözleşmesi satır 1-3, 7, seçim).
  52: 
  53:     Bu anahtarları DB (ingest_personal_listing) zaten sayar; burada yalnız içeriksiz log için kullanılır.
  54:     """
  55:     if status == "approved":
  56:         return "personal_auto_approved"
  57:     if status == "duplicate":
  58:         return "personal_duplicate"
  59:     if status == "unselected":
  60:         return "personal_unselected"
  61:     if status == "not_connected":
  62:         return "personal_not_connected"
  63:     if score is None:
  64:         return "personal_queued_noscore"
  65:     return "personal_queued"
  66: 
  67: 
  68: def _log_personal_outcome(key: str, agent_id: str) -> str:
  69:     """Sayaç anahtarını içeriksiz tek satır olarak loglar (mesaj metni/numara/jid yok)."""
  70:     logger.info("personal_outcome=%s agent=%s", key, agent_id)
  71:     return key
  72: 
  73: 
  74: def _count_personal(client, key: str, agent_id: str) -> str:
  75:     """Orchestrator'ın kendi sonucunu DB sayacına yazar (best-effort) ve içeriksiz loglar."""
  76:     bump_sidecar_counter(client, key)
  77:     return _log_personal_outcome(key, agent_id)
  78: 
  79: 
  80: def _handle_personal(
  81:     client,
  82:     agent_id: str,
  83:     payload: dict,
  84:     message_id: str,
  85:     text: str,
  86:     parsed: dict,
  87: ) -> str:
  88:     """Kişisel ilan yolu: owner yalnız agent_id'den gelir (AC-S3). Ortak insert/dedup'a girmez.
  89: 
  90:     Gönderen numarası record'a yazılmaz (AC-S4). Eşik: quality_score >= eşik ise approved,
  91:     aksi halde pending (admin kuyruğu). Yazma hatası yutulur; fallback yapılmaz (kısmi yazım yok).
  92:     """
  93:     score, _issues = score_listing(parsed)
  94:     # Eşik kararı tek yerde, DB'de verilir (ingest_personal_listing içinde, app_settings'ten).
  95:     # Python eşiği okumaz; record "status" alanı yalnız şekil uyumluluğu için sabit "pending"dir,
  96:     # DB bu alanı okumaz.
  97:     record = {
  98:         "source": "whatsapp",
  99:         "source_group": payload.get("group"),
 100:         "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
 101:         "source_message_id": message_id or None,
 102:         "raw_text": text,
 103:         "parsed": parsed,
 104:         "fingerprint": compute_fingerprint(parsed),
 105:         "quality_score": score,
 106:         "status": "pending",
 107:     }
 108:     try:
 109:         outcome = ingest_personal_listing(client, agent_id, record)
 110:     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - içeriksiz logla, worker'ı düşürme
 111:         logger.exception("Kişisel ilan yazılamadı (içerik loglanmaz)")
 112:         return _count_personal(client, "personal_write_error", agent_id)
 113: 
 114:     return _log_personal_outcome(_personal_outcome_key(outcome, score), agent_id)
 115: 
 116: 
 117: def handle_message(payload: dict) -> Optional[str]:
 118:     message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
 119:     text = (payload.get("text") or "").strip()
 120:     group = payload.get("group")
 121:     agent_id = payload.get("agentId")
 122: 
 123:     if not text:
 124:         return None
 125: 
 126:     try:
 127:         client = get_client()
 128:         key_manager = get_default_manager()
 129: 
 130:         if agent_id:
 131:             # KVKK: seçim önbelleği (sidecar, 60 sn) eski olabilir; metin parse'a (Gemini) gitmeden
 132:             # önce grubun bu emlakçı için HÂLÂ seçili olduğu DB'den doğrulanır.
 133:             try:
 134:                 selected = is_group_selected(client, agent_id, group)
 135:             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - pesimist: doğrulanamazsa parse etme, içerik loglanmaz
 136:                 logger.warning("Seçim doğrulanamadı, mesaj parse edilmedi (içerik loglanmaz)")
 137:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_selection_check_error", agent_id)
 138:             if not selected:
 139:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_unselected", agent_id)
 140:             try:
 141:                 parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
 142:             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - içerik loglanmaz, yalnız sayaç
 143:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_parse_error", agent_id)
 144:             if parsed is None:
 145:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_not_listing", agent_id)
 146:             return _handle_personal(client, agent_id, payload, message_id, text, parsed)
 147: 
 148:         parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
 149: 
 150:         if parsed is None:
 151:             logger.info("Mesaj ilan değil veya parse edilemedi, atlandı: %s", message_id)
 152:             return None
 153: 
 154:         fingerprint = compute_fingerprint(parsed)
 155:         if fingerprint_exists(client, fingerprint):
 156:             logger.info("Mükerrer ilan (fingerprint eşleşti), atlandı: %s", message_id)
 157:             return None
 158: 
 159:         score, issues = score_listing(parsed)
 160:         record = {
 161:             "source": "whatsapp",
 162:             "source_group": group,
 163:             "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
 164:             "source_message_id": message_id or None,
 165:             "raw_text": text,
 166:             "parsed": parsed,
 167:             "fingerprint": fingerprint,
 168:             "quality_score": score,
 169:             "status": "pending",
 170:             "owner_agent_id": None,
 171:         }
 172:         _inserted_row, inserted = insert_raw_listing(client, record)
 173:         if inserted:
 174:             logger.info(
 175:                 "raw_listings'e yazıldı: %s (skor=%.2f, auto_approve_uygun=%s, sorunlar=%s)",
 176:                 message_id,
 177:                 score,
 178:                 is_safe_to_auto_approve(score, float(os.getenv("QUALITY_GATE_THRESHOLD", "0.5"))),
 179:                 issues,
 180:             )
 181:         else:
 182:             logger.info(
 183:                 "Mükerrer ilan (fingerprint çakışması, DB seviyesinde atlandı): %s",
 184:                 message_id,
 185:             )
 186:     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - worker thread hatayı yutmasın, logla ve devam et
 187:         logger.exception("Mesaj işlenirken hata: %s", message_id)
 188:     finally:
 189:         with _active_ids_lock:
 190:             _active_ids.discard(message_id)
 191:     return None
 192: 
 193: 
 194: def _background_worker_loop(executor: ThreadPoolExecutor) -> None:
 195:     while True:
 196:         payload = message_queue.get()
 197:         executor.submit(handle_message, payload)
 198: 
 199: 
 200: class WebhookHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
 201:     def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - http.server API ismi
 202:         if self.path != "/baileys-webhook":
 203:             self.send_response(404)
 204:             self.end_headers()
 205:             return
 206: 
 207:         secret = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")
 208:         auth_header = self.headers.get("Authorization", "")
 209:         token = auth_header[len("Bearer "):] if auth_header.startswith("Bearer ") else ""
 210:         if not secret or not hmac.compare_digest(token, secret):
 211:             body = json.dumps({"error": "unauthorized"}).encode("utf-8")
 212:             self.send_response(401)
 213:             self.send_header("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer")
 214:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 215:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
 216:             self.end_headers()
 217:             self.wfile.write(body)
 218:             return
 219: 
 220:         length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
 221:         body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
 222:         try:
 223:             payload = json.loads(body or b"{}")
 224:         except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError):
 225:             # Body içeriği loglanmaz (PII riski).
 226:             logger.warning("Geçersiz webhook body (JSON/UTF-8 çözülemedi), 400 dönüldü.")
 227:             response = json.dumps({"error": "invalid_body"}).encode("utf-8")
 228:             self.send_response(400)
 229:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 230:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 231:             self.end_headers()
 232:             self.wfile.write(response)
 233:             return
 234: 
 235:         if not payload.get("text"):
 236:             # Metinsiz mesaj kuyruğa alınmaz; 200 ile "işlendi" denir (WhatsApp tekrar göndermesin).
 237:             response = b"{}"
 238:             self.send_response(200)
 239:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 240:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 241:             self.end_headers()
 242:             self.wfile.write(response)
 243:             return
 244: 
 245:         message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
 246:         with _active_ids_lock:
 247:             if message_id and message_id in _active_ids:
 248:                 self.send_response(200)
 249:                 self.end_headers()
 250:                 return
 251:             if message_id:
 252:                 _active_ids.add(message_id)
 253: 
 254:         message_queue.put(payload)
 255: 
 256:         self.send_response(200)
 257:         self.end_headers()
 258: 
 259:     def log_message(self, format: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: A002 - http.server imzası
 260:         logger.debug("%s - %s", self.address_string(), format % args)
 261: 
 262: 
 263: def run() -> None:
 264:     if not os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", ""):
 265:         logger.error("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET env değişkeni set edilmemiş, sunucu başlatılmıyor.")
 266:         sys.exit(1)
 267: 
 268:     host = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
 269:     port = int(os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_PORT", "8090"))
 270:     executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=MAX_WORKERS)
 271: 
 272:     worker_thread = threading.Thread(target=_background_worker_loop, args=(executor,), daemon=True)
 273:     worker_thread.start()
 274: 
 275:     server = HTTPServer((host, port), WebhookHandler)
 276:     logger.info("Scraper worker %s:%s/baileys-webhook adresinde dinliyor.", host, port)
 277:     server.serve_forever()
 278: 
 279: 
 280: if __name__ == "__main__":
 281:     run()
===== scraper/supabase_client.py =====
   1: # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
   2: """Scraper worker'ın Supabase'e yazımı için ince istemci.
   3: 
   4: Service-role key kullanır (RLS'i bypass eder) — bu dosya SADECE scraper
   5: worker'ın kendi sunucusunda/container'ında çalışır, tarayıcıya asla gönderilmez.
   6: """
   7: 
   8: import logging
   9: import os
  10: import re
  11: from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple
  12: 
  13: from postgrest.exceptions import APIError
  14: from supabase import Client, create_client
  15: 
  16: logger = logging.getLogger("scraper.supabase_client")
  17: 
  18: UNIQUE_VIOLATION_CODE = "23505"
  19: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.70
  20: THRESHOLD_KEY = "personal_auto_approve_threshold"
  21: # ingest_personal_listing dönüşü. "unselected": seçim DB'de yok; "not_connected": bağlantı yok
  22: # veya kesme istendi. İkisinde de hiçbir şey yazılmadı.
  23: # Sayaçların hepsi (personal_* ve threshold_fallback) DB tarafında, RPC içinde artırılır.
  24: PERSONAL_INGEST_STATUSES = ("approved", "pending", "duplicate", "unselected", "not_connected")
  25: # bump_sidecar_counter ile aynı kural (DB de ayrıca doğrular).
  26: SIDECAR_COUNTER_KEY_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z_]{1,40}$")
  27: 
  28: 
  29: def get_client() -> Client:
  30:     url = os.environ["SUPABASE_URL"]
  31:     key = os.environ["SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"]
  32:     return create_client(url, key)
  33: 
  34: 
  35: def insert_raw_listing(client: Client, record: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], bool]:
  36:     """raw_listings tablosuna tek kayıt ekler (atomik, unique-violation'a karşı güvenli).
  37: 
  38:     record alanları: source, source_group, source_message_id, raw_text,
  39:     parsed (dict), fingerprint, quality_score.
  40:     source_message_id üzerindeki unique index aynı mesajın iki kez
  41:     eklenmesini veritabanı seviyesinde engeller (dedup'ın son savunma hattı).
  42:     fingerprint kolonunda da (status <> 'rejected' koşuluyla) PARTIAL bir
  43:     unique index var (bkz. migration 0007) — paralel worker'ların aynı
  44:     fingerprint'i neredeyse aynı anda insert etmeye çalıştığı race condition'ı
  45:     DB seviyesinde engeller. Bu fonksiyon, o unique-violation'ı (23505)
  46:     try/except ile yakalayıp sessizce `(None, False)` döndürerek atomikliği
  47:     sağlar; 23505 DIŞINDA bir hata (gerçek DB/bağlantı/yetki hatası) YUKARI
  48:     FIRLATILIR, yutulmaz.
  49: 
  50:     Dönüş: (kayıt veya None, inserted: bool).
  51:     """
  52:     try:
  53:         result = client.table("raw_listings").insert(record).execute()
  54:     except APIError as error:
  55:         if error.code == UNIQUE_VIOLATION_CODE:
  56:             return None, False
  57:         raise
  58:     return (result.data[0] if result.data else {}), True
  59: 
  60: 
  61: def fingerprint_exists(client: Client, fingerprint: str) -> bool:
  62:     """Ortak havuz (owner_agent_id null) için dedup. Kişisel kayıtlar bu kontrolü tetiklemez (AC-11)."""
  63:     result = (
  64:         client.table("raw_listings")
  65:         .select("id")
  66:         .eq("fingerprint", fingerprint)
  67:         .is_("owner_agent_id", "null")
  68:         .limit(1)
  69:         .execute()
  70:     )
  71:     return bool(result.data)
  72: 
  73: 
  74: def is_group_selected(client: Client, agent_id: str, group_jid: Optional[str]) -> bool:
  75:     """Grup, emlakçının DB'deki güncel seçimlerinde mi? (service-role, wa_group_selection)
  76: 
  77:     Sidecar seçim önbelleği 60 sn eski olabilir; bu yüzden kişisel yolda parse'tan ÖNCE
  78:     DB'ye sorulur. Seçili değilse mesaj metni üçüncü tarafa (Gemini) gönderilmez (KVKK).
  79:     Sorgu hatası YUKARI FIRLATILIR; çağıran taraf pesimist davranır (parse etmez).
  80:     group_jid boşsa sorgu yapılmadan False döner.
  81:     """
  82:     if not group_jid:
  83:         return False
  84:     result = (
  85:         client.table("wa_group_selection")
  86:         .select("group_jid")
  87:         .eq("agent_id", agent_id)
  88:         .eq("group_jid", group_jid)
  89:         .limit(1)
  90:         .execute()
  91:     )
  92:     return bool(result.data)
  93: 
  94: 
  95: def get_threshold(client: Client) -> Tuple[float, bool]:
  96:     """app_settings'ten otomatik onay eşiğini okur.
  97: 
  98:     Dönüş: (eşik, fallback). Okuma hatası, eksik satır veya 0-1 dışı/sayı olmayan
  99:     değerde (0.70, True) döner: eşik belirsizse ilan admin kuyruğunda kalır.
 100:     """
 101:     try:
 102:         result = (
 103:             client.table("app_settings")
 104:             .select("value")
 105:             .eq("key", THRESHOLD_KEY)
 106:             .limit(1)
 107:             .execute()
 108:         )
 109:         rows = result.data
 110:     except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - eşik okunamazsa varsayılana düş, akış durmaz
 111:         logger.warning("Eşik okunamadı, varsayılan kullanılıyor: %s", type(error).__name__)
 112:         return DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, True
 113: 
 114:     if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
 115:         logger.warning("Eşik ayarı bulunamadı, varsayılan kullanılıyor")
 116:         return DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, True
 117: 
 118:     value = rows[0].get("value") if isinstance(rows[0], dict) else None
 119:     if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or not 0.0 <= value <= 1.0:
 120:         logger.warning("Geçersiz eşik değeri, varsayılan kullanılıyor")
 121:         return DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, True
 122:     return float(value), False
 123: 
 124: 
 125: def ingest_personal_listing(client: Client, agent_id: str, record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
 126:     """Kişisel ilanı tek transaction'da yazar (service-role RPC).
 127: 
 128:     owner yalnız agent_id'den gelir; record içindeki owner alanı RPC'ye taşınmaz.
 129:     Bağlantı ve seçim kontrolü DB'de yapılır (kilitli): bağlı değil -> "not_connected";
 130:     seçili olmayan grup -> "unselected". İkisinde de hiçbir şey yazılmaz.
 131:     Dönüş: "approved" | "pending" | "duplicate" | "unselected" | "not_connected".
 132:     Beklenmeyen dönüş ValueError fırlatır.
 133:     """
 134:     result = client.rpc(
 135:         "ingest_personal_listing",
 136:         {"p_agent_id": agent_id, "p_record": record},
 137:     ).execute()
 138:     status = result.data
 139:     if status not in PERSONAL_INGEST_STATUSES:
 140:         raise ValueError("ingest_personal_listing beklenmeyen sonuç döndürdü")
 141:     return status
 142: 
 143: 
 144: def bump_sidecar_counter(client: Client, key: str) -> bool:
 145:     """Orchestrator sayacını sidecar_heartbeat.counters.orchestrator[key] içinde +1 yapar.
 146: 
 147:     Best-effort: RPC hatası yutulur, yalnız anahtar adı ve hata tipi loglanır (içerik yok).
 148:     Dönüş: başarı bayrağı. Geçersiz anahtar RPC'ye gönderilmez.
 149:     """
 150:     if not isinstance(key, str) or not SIDECAR_COUNTER_KEY_RE.match(key):
 151:         logger.warning("Geçersiz sayaç anahtarı, sayılmadı: %r", key if isinstance(key, str) else type(key).__name__)
 152:         return False
 153:     try:
 154:         client.rpc("bump_sidecar_counter", {"p_key": key}).execute()
 155:     except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - sayaç yazımı akışı durdurmaz
 156:         logger.warning("Sayaç artırılamadı (%s): %s", key, type(error).__name__)
 157:         return False
 158:     return True
===== web/src/lib/push/targets.ts =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: 
   3: /**
   4:  * Cron gönderim hedefleri ve yardımcı sorgular. Yalnız service_role istemcisiyle
   5:  * çağrılır (cron route). İstemci girdisi almaz.
   6:  */
   7: 
   8: /** Erişimi olan abonelik durumları (AC-3). */
   9: export const ACCESS_STATUSES = ["active", "trialing", "past_due"] as const;
  10: 
  11: const PAGE_SIZE = 1000;
  12: 
  13: export type PushTarget = {
  14:   id: string;
  15:   agent_id: string;
  16:   endpoint: string;
  17:   p256dh: string;
  18:   auth: string;
  19:   /** Yeni ilan sayımının başlangıcı: coalesce(last_notified_at, created_at). */
  20:   since: string;
  21: };
  22: 
  23: type PushRow = {
  24:   id: string;
  25:   agent_id: string;
  26:   endpoint: string;
  27:   p256dh: string;
  28:   auth: string;
  29:   created_at: string;
  30:   last_notified_at: string | null;
  31: };
  32: 
  33: type PageResult<T> = { data: T[] | null; error: unknown };
  34: 
  35: /**
  36:  * PostgREST varsayılan olarak 1000 satır döndürür; tüm sayfaları sırayla toplar.
  37:  * `order("id")` ile sayfalar kararlı tutulur.
  38:  */
  39: async function selectAll<T>(
  40:   run: (from: number, to: number) => PromiseLike<PageResult<T>>,
  41: ): Promise<PageResult<T>> {
  42:   const out: T[] = [];
  43:   for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE_SIZE) {
  44:     const { data, error } = await run(from, from + PAGE_SIZE - 1);
  45:     if (error) return { data: null, error };
  46:     const page = data ?? [];
  47:     out.push(...page);
  48:     if (page.length < PAGE_SIZE) return { data: out, error: null };
  49:   }
  50: }
  51: 
  52: /**
  53:  * Erişimi olan (subscriptions: active/trialing/past_due ve current_period_end > now)
  54:  * agent'ların push abonelik satırlarını döner. Erişimi olmayan satırlar silinmez,
  55:  * yalnız burada atlanır (AC-3).
  56:  */
  57: export async function loadTargets(
  58:   admin: SupabaseClient,
  59: ): Promise<{ data: PushTarget[] | null; error: unknown }> {
  60:   const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  61: 
  62:   const subs = await selectAll<{ id: string; agent_id: string }>((from, to) =>
  63:     admin
  64:       .from("subscriptions")
  65:       .select("id, agent_id")
  66:       .in("status", [...ACCESS_STATUSES])
  67:       .gt("current_period_end", nowIso)
  68:       .order("id")
  69:       .range(from, to),
  70:   );
  71:   if (subs.error) return { data: null, error: subs.error };
  72: 
  73:   const accessible = new Set((subs.data ?? []).map((s) => s.agent_id));
  74:   if (accessible.size === 0) return { data: [], error: null };
  75: 
  76:   const rows = await selectAll<PushRow>((from, to) =>
  77:     admin
  78:       .from("push_subscriptions")
  79:       .select("id, agent_id, endpoint, p256dh, auth, created_at, last_notified_at")
  80:       .order("id")
  81:       .range(from, to),
  82:   );
  83:   if (rows.error) return { data: null, error: rows.error };
  84: 
  85:   const data: PushTarget[] = (rows.data ?? [])
  86:     .filter((r) => accessible.has(r.agent_id))
  87:     .map((r) => ({
  88:       id: r.id,
  89:       agent_id: r.agent_id,
  90:       endpoint: r.endpoint,
  91:       p256dh: r.p256dh,
  92:       auth: r.auth,
  93:       since: r.last_notified_at ?? r.created_at,
  94:     }));
  95: 
  96:   return { data, error: null };
  97: }
  98: 
  99: const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
 100: 
 101: /**
 102:  * `since` anından sonra onaylanmış ilan sayısı, hedef agent'a görünür olanlar:
 103:  * ortak havuz (owner_agent_id is null) ve agent'ın KENDİ kişisel ilanları (AC-S1).
 104:  * Başka emlakçının kişisel ilanı bu agent'a sayılmaz.
 105:  */
 106: export async function countNewListings(
 107:   admin: SupabaseClient,
 108:   since: string,
 109:   agentId: string,
 110: ): Promise<{ count: number; error: unknown }> {
 111:   if (!UUID_RE.test(agentId)) return { count: 0, error: new Error("invalid agent_id") };
 112:   const { count, error } = await admin
 113:     .from("listings")
 114:     .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
 115:     .gt("created_at", since)
 116:     .or(`owner_agent_id.is.null,owner_agent_id.eq.${agentId}`);
 117:   if (error) return { count: 0, error };
 118:   return { count: count ?? 0, error: null };
 119: }
 120: 
 121: /** Başarılı gönderimden sonra yalnız o satırın last_notified_at değerini yazar. */
 122: export async function markNotified(
 123:   admin: SupabaseClient,
 124:   subscriptionId: string,
 125:   notifiedAt: string,
 126: ): Promise<{ error: unknown }> {
 127:   const { error } = await admin
 128:     .from("push_subscriptions")
 129:     .update({ last_notified_at: notifiedAt })
 130:     .eq("id", subscriptionId);
 131:   return { error: error ?? null };
 132: }
 133: 
 134: /** 404/410 (push servisi aboneliği geçersiz saydı) → satırı id ile siler. */
 135: export async function removeSubscription(
 136:   admin: SupabaseClient,
 137:   subscriptionId: string,
 138: ): Promise<{ error: unknown }> {
 139:   const { error } = await admin
 140:     .from("push_subscriptions")
 141:     .delete()
 142:     .eq("id", subscriptionId);
 143:   return { error: error ?? null };
 144: }
===== web/src/app/api/cron/push/route.ts =====
   1: import { timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
   2: 
   3: import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
   4: import webpush from "web-push";
   5: 
   6: import { getVapidConfig } from "@/lib/push/config";
   7: import { isAllowedPushEndpoint, isValidKey } from "@/lib/push/endpoint";
   8: import { buildPayload } from "@/lib/push/payload";
   9: import {
  10:   countNewListings,
  11:   loadTargets,
  12:   markNotified,
  13:   removeSubscription,
  14: } from "@/lib/push/targets";
  15: import type { PushTarget } from "@/lib/push/targets";
  16: import { createServiceRoleClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
  17: 
  18: /** Aynı anda Supabase / push servisine giden istek üst sınırı (1000 abonelikte < 10 sn hedefi). */
  19: const CONCURRENCY = 20;
  20: 
  21: type Summary = {
  22:   sent: number;
  23:   failed: number;
  24:   removed: number;
  25:   skipped: "disabled" | "no_subscribers" | "nothing_new" | null;
  26: };
  27: 
  28: function isAuthorized(header: string | null): boolean {
  29:   // Fail-closed: CRON_SECRET yoksa hiçbir istek geçmez (AC-S3).
  30:   const secret = process.env.CRON_SECRET;
  31:   if (!secret || !header) return false;
  32:   const expected = `Bearer ${secret}`;
  33:   if (header.length !== expected.length) return false;
  34:   return timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(header), Buffer.from(expected));
  35: }
  36: 
  37: function emptySummary(skipped: Summary["skipped"]): Summary {
  38:   return { sent: 0, failed: 0, removed: 0, skipped };
  39: }
  40: 
  41: async function mapLimit<T, R>(
  42:   items: T[],
  43:   limit: number,
  44:   fn: (item: T) => Promise<R>,
  45: ): Promise<R[]> {
  46:   const results: R[] = new Array(items.length);
  47:   let next = 0;
  48:   const workers = Array.from({ length: Math.min(limit, items.length) }, async () => {
  49:     while (next < items.length) {
  50:       const index = next++;
  51:       results[index] = await fn(items[index]);
  52:     }
  53:   });
  54:   await Promise.all(workers);
  55:   return results;
  56: }
  57: 
  58: type Outcome = "sent" | "failed" | "removed";
  59: 
  60: function statusOf(err: unknown): number | undefined {
  61:   return (err as { statusCode?: number } | null)?.statusCode;
  62: }
  63: 
  64: export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  65:   if (!isAuthorized(request.headers.get("authorization"))) {
  66:     return NextResponse.json({ error: "unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  67:   }
  68: 
  69:   const vapid = getVapidConfig();
  70:   if (!vapid) {
  71:     console.warn("[push] cron devre dışı: VAPID env eksik");
  72:     return NextResponse.json(emptySummary("disabled"));
  73:   }
  74:   webpush.setVapidDetails(vapid.subject, vapid.publicKey, vapid.privateKey);
  75: 
  76:   const admin = createServiceRoleClient();
  77:   // Pencere işaretçisi: bu çalışmanın başlangıcı. Gönderilen satırların last_notified_at'i bu değer olur.
  78:   const runAt = new Date().toISOString();
  79: 
  80:   const loaded = await loadTargets(admin);
  81:   if (loaded.error || !loaded.data) {
  82:     console.error("[push] cron db_failed: loadTargets", loaded.error);
  83:     return NextResponse.json({ error: "db_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  84:   }
  85:   const targets: PushTarget[] = loaded.data;
  86:   if (targets.length === 0) {
  87:     return NextResponse.json(emptySummary("no_subscribers"));
  88:   }
  89: 
  90:   // Önce tüm sayımlar; herhangi biri hata verirse hiçbir cihaza gönderim yapılmaz.
  91:   const counts = await mapLimit(targets, CONCURRENCY, (t) => countNewListings(admin, t.since, t.agent_id));
  92:   const countFailure = counts.find((c) => c.error);
  93:   if (countFailure) {
  94:     console.error("[push] cron db_failed: countNewListings", countFailure.error);
  95:     return NextResponse.json({ error: "db_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  96:   }
  97: 
  98:   const pending = targets
  99:     .map((target, i) => ({ target, count: counts[i].count }))
 100:     .filter((p) => p.count > 0);
 101:   if (pending.length === 0) {
 102:     return NextResponse.json(emptySummary("nothing_new"));
 103:   }
 104: 
 105:   const outcomes = await mapLimit(pending, CONCURRENCY, async ({ target, count }): Promise<Outcome> => {
 106:     // İkinci katman (SSRF): DB CHECK kısıtına ek olarak göndermeden önce yeniden doğrula.
 107:     // Geçersiz satıra istek atılmaz; satır silinir. Endpoint loglanmaz.
 108:     if (!isAllowedPushEndpoint(target.endpoint) || !isValidKey(target.p256dh) || !isValidKey(target.auth)) {
 109:       console.warn("[push] invalid endpoint removed", { id: target.id });
 110:       const { error } = await removeSubscription(admin, target.id);
 111:       if (error) console.error("[push] removeSubscription failed", error);
 112:       return "removed";
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     const subscription = {
 116:       endpoint: target.endpoint,
 117:       keys: { p256dh: target.p256dh, auth: target.auth },
 118:     };
 119:     try {
 120:       await webpush.sendNotification(subscription, JSON.stringify(buildPayload(count)));
 121:     } catch (err) {
 122:       const status = statusOf(err);
 123:       if (status === 404 || status === 410) {
 124:         // Push servisi aboneliği geçersiz saydı (AC-7).
 125:         const { error } = await removeSubscription(admin, target.id);
 126:         if (error) console.error("[push] removeSubscription failed", error);
 127:         return "removed";
 128:       }
 129:       // 429/5xx: satır tutulur, last_notified_at değişmez; sonraki pencerede tekrar sayılır.
 130:       console.warn("[push] send failed", { id: target.id, status });
 131:       return "failed";
 132:     }
 133: 
 134:     const { error } = await markNotified(admin, target.id, runAt);
 135:     if (error) console.error("[push] markNotified failed", error);
 136:     return "sent";
 137:   });
 138: 
 139:   const summary: Summary = {
 140:     sent: outcomes.filter((o) => o === "sent").length,
 141:     failed: outcomes.filter((o) => o === "failed").length,
 142:     removed: outcomes.filter((o) => o === "removed").length,
 143:     skipped: null,
 144:   };
 145:   console.info("[push] cron", summary);
 146:   return NextResponse.json(summary);
 147: }
===== web/src/app/agent/whatsapp/group-picker.tsx =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import { useState } from "react";
   4: 
   5: import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
   6: import type { WaGroupsOk, WaSetGroupsResult } from "@/lib/whatsapp/groups";
   7: 
   8: // EML-107 AC-1, AC-2, AC-10. Yalnız seçim UI'ı; kaydetme onSave prop'undan (server action) gelir.
   9: // Bağlantı connected değilse liste salt-okunur ve "Yeniden bağlan" uyarısı gösterilir.
  10: 
  11: const SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  12:   too_many: "En fazla 20 grup",
  13:   unknown_group: "Liste güncel değil, yenile",
  14:   unauthorized: "Oturumunuz sona erdi, tekrar giriş yapın.",
  15: };
  16: const DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR = "Kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.";
  17: 
  18: type Notice = { kind: "ok" | "error"; text: string } | null;
  19: 
  20: export type GroupPickerProps = {
  21:   initial: WaGroupsOk;
  22:   onSave: (jids: string[]) => Promise<WaSetGroupsResult>;
  23: };
  24: 
  25: export function GroupPicker({ initial, onSave }: GroupPickerProps) {
  26:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(
  27:     () => new Set(initial.groups.filter((g) => g.selected).map((g) => g.jid)),
  28:   );
  29:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  30:   const [notice, setNotice] = useState<Notice>(null);
  31:   // Kaydetmede not_connected dönerse, sayfa yenilenene kadar salt-okunur kalır.
  32:   const [notConnected, setNotConnected] = useState(false);
  33: 
  34:   const readonly = initial.status !== "connected" || notConnected;
  35: 
  36:   if (initial.status === "none") {
  37:     return (
  38:       <section aria-labelledby="wa-groups-title" className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
  39:         <h2 id="wa-groups-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
  40:           Grup seçimi
  41:         </h2>
  42:         <p className="text-sm text-ink-900">{"Önce WhatsApp'ını bağla"}</p>
  43:       </section>
  44:     );
  45:   }
  46: 
  47:   const awaitingSync = initial.groups.length === 0 && initial.groups_synced_at === null;
  48: 
  49:   function toggle(jid: string) {
  50:     setNotice(null);
  51:     setSelected((prev) => {
  52:       const next = new Set(prev);
  53:       if (next.has(jid)) next.delete(jid);
  54:       else if (next.size < initial.max) next.add(jid);
  55:       return next;
  56:     });
  57:   }
  58: 
  59:   async function handleSave() {
  60:     const jids = initial.groups.filter((g) => selected.has(g.jid)).map((g) => g.jid);
  61:     setBusy(true);
  62:     setNotice(null);
  63:     const res = await onSave(jids);
  64:     setBusy(false);
  65:     if (res.ok) {
  66:       setNotice(
  67:         res.selected === 0
  68:           ? { kind: "ok", text: "Hiçbir grup okunmuyor" }
  69:           : { kind: "ok", text: "Kaydedildi" },
  70:       );
  71:       return;
  72:     }
  73:     if (res.error === "not_connected") {
  74:       setNotConnected(true);
  75:       return;
  76:     }
  77:     setNotice({ kind: "error", text: SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES[res.error] ?? DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR });
  78:   }
  79: 
  80:   const primaryButton =
  81:     "rounded-md bg-terracotta-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:opacity-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-ink-900 disabled:opacity-50";
  82: 
  83:   return (
  84:     <section aria-labelledby="wa-groups-title" className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
  85:       <h2 id="wa-groups-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
  86:         Grup seçimi
  87:       </h2>
  88: 
  89:       {readonly && (
  90:         <Alert variant="error">
  91:           <p>
  92:             WhatsApp bağlantınız aktif değil; grup seçimi salt okunur. <strong>Yeniden bağlan</strong>
  93:           </p>
  94:         </Alert>
  95:       )}
  96: 
  97:       {awaitingSync ? (
  98:         <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Gruplar yükleniyor…</p>
  99:       ) : (
 100:         <>
 101:           <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{`${selected.size}/${initial.max}`}</p>
 102:           <ul className="space-y-2">
 103:             {initial.groups.map((group) => {
 104:               const checked = selected.has(group.jid);
 105:               const full = !checked && selected.size >= initial.max;
 106:               return (
 107:                 <li key={group.jid}>
 108:                   <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-ink-900">
 109:                     <input
 110:                       type="checkbox"
 111:                       className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0"
 112:                       checked={checked}
 113:                       disabled={readonly || busy || full}
 114:                       onChange={() => toggle(group.jid)}
 115:                     />
 116:                     <span className="min-w-0 break-words">{group.name ?? "(adsız grup)"}</span>
 117:                   </label>
 118:                 </li>
 119:               );
 120:             })}
 121:           </ul>
 122: 
 123:           {initial.groups.length > 0 && (
 124:             <button
 125:               type="button"
 126:               onClick={handleSave}
 127:               disabled={readonly || busy}
 128:               className={primaryButton}
 129:             >
 130:               Kaydet
 131:             </button>
 132:           )}
 133:         </>
 134:       )}
 135: 
 136:       {notice && (
 137:         <Alert variant={notice.kind === "ok" ? "success" : "error"}>
 138:           <p>{notice.text}</p>
 139:         </Alert>
 140:       )}
 141:     </section>
 142:   );
 143: }
===== web/src/app/agent/whatsapp/wa-panel.tsx =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import QRCode from "qrcode";
   4: import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
   5: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
   6: 
   7: import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
   8: import type { WaGroupsResult } from "@/lib/whatsapp/groups";
   9: import { WA_CONSENT_VERSION, type WaStatusResult, type WaStatusView } from "@/lib/whatsapp/status";
  10: 
  11: import {
  12:   acceptWaConsent,
  13:   getMyWaStatus,
  14:   requestWaAction,
  15:   setMyWaGroupSelection,
  16: } from "./actions";
  17: import { GroupPicker } from "./group-picker";
  18: 
  19: // EML-106 / EML-104 AC-1, AC-2, AC-3, AC-5..AC-11. Hiçbir agent_id istemciden gönderilmez (AC-S1).
  20: // QR yalnız get_my_wa_status yanıtından gelir (AC-S2); qrcode kütüphanesiyle SVG'ye çevrilir.
  21: 
  22: const POLL_MS = 2000;
  23: /** Kaldırma sonrası sidecar işlemezse bu kadar poll'dan sonra hata gösterilir (~60 sn). */
  24: const DISCONNECT_MAX_POLLS = 30;
  25: 
  26: const CONSENT_TEXT =
  27:   "WhatsApp resmi olmayan bağlantıları kısıtlayabilir; numaranız kapatılabilir. Sistem numaranızdan hiçbir mesaj göndermez, yalnızca seçtiğiniz gruplardaki ilanları okur.";
  28: const CONSENT_LABEL = "Riski anladım ve kabul ediyorum.";
  29: 
  30: const DEFAULT_ERROR = "Bir hata oluştu, tekrar deneyin.";
  31: const LOGGED_OUT_MESSAGE = "Bağlantı telefondan kaldırıldı, yeniden bağlayabilirsiniz.";
  32: 
  33: const ACTION_ERROR_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  34:   subscription_required: "Bu özellik için aktif abonelik gerekir.",
  35:   rate_limited: "Çok fazla deneme, 10 dk sonra tekrar deneyin.",
  36:   rpc_failed: DEFAULT_ERROR,
  37: };
  38: 
  39: const STATUS_ERROR_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  40:   sidecar_timeout: "Servis şu an yanıt vermiyor, biraz sonra tekrar deneyin.",
  41:   no_qr_generated: "Bağlantı başlatılamadı, tekrar deneyin.",
  42:   capacity: "Şu an yeni bağlantı açılamıyor.",
  43:   creds_persist_failed: "Bağlantı kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.",
  44:   sidecar_misconfigured: "Servis şu an kullanılamıyor.",
  45: };
  46: 
  47: const DISCONNECTED_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  48:   qr_expired: "Süre doldu, tekrar deneyin.",
  49:   replaced: "Bağlantı başka bir yerde açıldı.",
  50:   subscription_inactive: "Aboneliğiniz aktif değil.",
  51: };
  52: 
  53: // Panel yalnız yerel "kaldırılıyor" ara durumunu ekler; diğerleri sunucu şekli.
  54: type ViewStatus = WaStatusView | { status: "disconnecting" };
  55: 
  56: type RetryKind = "consent" | "connect" | "disconnect";
  57: type PanelError = { message: string; retry?: RetryKind } | null;
  58: 
  59: export type WaPanelProps = {
  60:   initialStatus: WaStatusView;
  61:   consentAccepted: boolean;
  62:   /** EML-107: grup listesi (sunucu, get_my_wa_groups). Yoksa grup bölümü gösterilmez. */
  63:   groups?: WaGroupsResult;
  64: };
  65: 
  66: /** Hata/durum mesajı gereken durumlar; "Bağla" yerine "Tekrar dene" gösterilir. */
  67: function statusMessage(status: ViewStatus): string | null {
  68:   switch (status.status) {
  69:     case "logged_out":
  70:       return LOGGED_OUT_MESSAGE;
  71:     case "disconnected": {
  72:       const known = status.error_code ? DISCONNECTED_MESSAGES[status.error_code] : undefined;
  73:       return known ?? "Bağlantı kesildi, tekrar bağlayabilirsiniz.";
  74:     }
  75:     case "error": {
  76:       const known = status.error_code ? STATUS_ERROR_MESSAGES[status.error_code] : undefined;
  77:       return known ?? DEFAULT_ERROR;
  78:     }
  79:     default:
  80:       return null;
  81:   }
  82: }
  83: 
  84: function actionErrorMessage(code: string): string {
  85:   return ACTION_ERROR_MESSAGES[code] ?? DEFAULT_ERROR;
  86: }
  87: 
  88: // Kaldırma sırasında sidecar henüz işlemediyse "connected" görünür; yalnız sonuç durumları kabul edilir.
  89: const DISCONNECT_TERMINAL = new Set(["none", "disconnected", "error", "logged_out"]);
  90: 
  91: function applyPoll(prev: ViewStatus, res: WaStatusResult, timedOut: boolean): ViewStatus {
  92:   if (!res.ok) return { status: "error", error_code: null };
  93:   if (prev.status === "disconnecting") {
  94:     if (timedOut) return { status: "error", error_code: "sidecar_timeout" };
  95:     return DISCONNECT_TERMINAL.has(res.status) ? res : prev;
  96:   }
  97:   return res;
  98: }
  99: 
 100: export function WaPanel({ initialStatus, consentAccepted, groups }: WaPanelProps) {
 101:   const [status, setStatus] = useState<ViewStatus>(initialStatus);
 102:   const [consent, setConsent] = useState(consentAccepted);
 103:   const [ticked, setTicked] = useState(false);
 104:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
 105:   const [error, setError] = useState<PanelError>(null);
 106:   const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
 107:   const [qrImage, setQrImage] = useState<{ value: string; svg: string } | null>(null);
 108:   const pollCountRef = useRef(0);
 109:   const router = useRouter();
 110:   const lastStatusRef = useRef<string>(initialStatus.status);
 111: 
 112:   const isPolling =
 113:     status.status === "pending" || status.status === "qr" || status.status === "disconnecting";
 114: 
 115:   // Bağlantı durumu değiştiğinde (ör. connected) sunucu bileşeni yeniden çalışır ve grup listesi
 116:   // tazelenir; mount'taki ilk durum değişimi sayılmaz.
 117:   useEffect(() => {
 118:     if (lastStatusRef.current === status.status) return;
 119:     lastStatusRef.current = status.status;
 120:     router.refresh();
 121:   }, [status.status, router]);
 122: 
 123:   // 2 sn poll: yalnız bekleme/QR/kaldırma sürerken.
 124:   useEffect(() => {
 125:     if (!isPolling) return;
 126:     const id = setInterval(async () => {
 127:       pollCountRef.current += 1;
 128:       const timedOut = pollCountRef.current > DISCONNECT_MAX_POLLS;
 129:       const res = await getMyWaStatus();
 130:       setStatus((prev) => applyPoll(prev, res, timedOut));
 131:     }, POLL_MS);
 132:     return () => clearInterval(id);
 133:   }, [isPolling]);
 134: 
 135:   const qrValue = status.status === "qr" ? status.qr : null;
 136: 
 137:   // QR SVG: qrcode kütüphanesi (yalnız kendi üretimi; kullanıcı girdisi içermez).
 138:   useEffect(() => {
 139:     if (qrValue === null) return;
 140:     let cancelled = false;
 141:     QRCode.toString(qrValue, { type: "svg", margin: 1, width: 240, errorCorrectionLevel: "M" })
 142:       .then((svg) => {
 143:         if (!cancelled) setQrImage({ value: qrValue, svg });
 144:       })
 145:       .catch(() => {
 146:         if (!cancelled) setError({ message: "QR kodu gösterilemedi, tekrar deneyin.", retry: "connect" });
 147:       });
 148:     return () => {
 149:       cancelled = true;
 150:     };
 151:   }, [qrValue]);
 152: 
 153:   async function handleAccept() {
 154:     if (!ticked) return;
 155:     setBusy(true);
 156:     setError(null);
 157:     const res = await acceptWaConsent(WA_CONSENT_VERSION);
 158:     setBusy(false);
 159:     if (res.ok) {
 160:       setConsent(true);
 161:       setTicked(false);
 162:       return;
 163:     }
 164:     setError({ message: "Onay kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.", retry: "consent" });
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   async function handleConnect() {
 168:     setBusy(true);
 169:     setError(null);
 170:     const res = await requestWaAction("connect");
 171:     setBusy(false);
 172:     if (!res.ok) {
 173:       if (res.error === "consent_required") {
 174:         // Sunucu onayı görmedi: onay kutusu yeniden gösterilir, Tekrar dene sunulmaz.
 175:         setConsent(false);
 176:         setTicked(false);
 177:         setError({ message: "Devam etmek için riski onaylamanız gerekir." });
 178:         return;
 179:       }
 180:       setError({ message: actionErrorMessage(res.error), retry: "connect" });
 181:       return;
 182:     }
 183:     pollCountRef.current = 0;
 184:     setStatus({ status: "pending" });
 185:   }
 186: 
 187:   async function handleDisconnect() {
 188:     setConfirmOpen(false);
 189:     setBusy(true);
 190:     setError(null);
 191:     const res = await requestWaAction("disconnect");
 192:     setBusy(false);
 193:     if (!res.ok) {
 194:       setError({ message: actionErrorMessage(res.error), retry: "disconnect" });
 195:       return;
 196:     }
 197:     pollCountRef.current = 0;
 198:     setStatus({ status: "disconnecting" });
 199:   }
 200: 
 201:   function retry(kind: RetryKind) {
 202:     if (kind === "consent") void handleAccept();
 203:     else if (kind === "connect") void handleConnect();
 204:     else void handleDisconnect();
 205:   }
 206: 
 207:   const statusError = statusMessage(status);
 208:   const canConnect =
 209:     status.status === "none" || status.status === "error" || status.status === "disconnected" || status.status === "logged_out";
 210:   const primaryButton =
 211:     "rounded-md bg-terracotta-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:opacity-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-ink-900 disabled:opacity-50";
 212:   const secondaryButton =
 213:     "rounded-md border border-mist-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink-900 hover:bg-mist-200 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-ink-900 disabled:opacity-60";
 214: 
 215:   const qrSvg = qrValue !== null && qrImage?.value === qrValue ? qrImage.svg : null;
 216: 
 217:   return (
 218:     <section aria-labelledby="wa-panel-title" className="mt-6 max-w-xl space-y-4">
 219:       <h2 id="wa-panel-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
 220:         WhatsApp bağlantısı
 221:       </h2>
 222: 
 223:       {!consent && (
 224:         <div className="space-y-3 rounded-md border border-mist-200 p-4 text-sm text-ink-900">
 225:           <p>{CONSENT_TEXT}</p>
 226:           <label className="flex items-start gap-2">
 227:             <input
 228:               type="checkbox"
 229:               className="mt-1"
 230:               checked={ticked}
 231:               onChange={(e) => setTicked(e.target.checked)}
 232:             />
 233:             <span>{CONSENT_LABEL}</span>
 234:           </label>
 235:           <button
 236:             type="button"
 237:             onClick={handleAccept}
 238:             disabled={!ticked || busy}
 239:             className={primaryButton}
 240:           >
 241:             Onayla
 242:           </button>
 243:         </div>
 244:       )}
 245: 
 246:       <div aria-live="polite" className="space-y-3 text-sm text-ink-900">
 247:         {status.status === "none" && <p>Bağlı değil</p>}
 248: 
 249:         {status.status === "pending" && <p>Hazırlanıyor…</p>}
 250: 
 251:         {status.status === "disconnecting" && <p>Bağlantı kaldırılıyor…</p>}
 252: 
 253:         {status.status === "qr" && (
 254:           <div className="space-y-3">
 255:             {qrSvg ? (
 256:               <div
 257:                 role="img"
 258:                 aria-label="WhatsApp QR kodu"
 259:                 className="inline-block rounded bg-white p-3"
 260:                 dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: qrSvg }}
 261:               />
 262:             ) : (
 263:               <p>QR hazırlanıyor…</p>
 264:             )}
 265:             <p className="text-slate-500">
 266:               Telefonunuzda WhatsApp → Ayarlar → Bağlı cihazlar → Cihaz bağla yolunu açıp bu kodu okutun.
 267:             </p>
 268:           </div>
 269:         )}
 270: 
 271:         {status.status === "connected" && (
 272:           <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
 273:             <p>{status.phone_masked ? `Bağlı: ${status.phone_masked}` : "Bağlı"}</p>
 274:             <button
 275:               type="button"
 276:               onClick={() => setConfirmOpen(true)}
 277:               disabled={busy}
 278:               className={secondaryButton}
 279:             >
 280:               Bağlantıyı kaldır
 281:             </button>
 282:           </div>
 283:         )}
 284:       </div>
 285: 
 286:       {error && (
 287:         <Alert variant="error">
 288:           <p>{error.message}</p>
 289:           {error.retry && (
 290:             <button
 291:               type="button"
 292:               onClick={() => retry(error.retry as RetryKind)}
 293:               disabled={busy}
 294:               className={`${secondaryButton} mt-2`}
 295:             >
 296:               Tekrar dene
 297:             </button>
 298:           )}
 299:         </Alert>
 300:       )}
 301: 
 302:       {!error && statusError && (
 303:         <Alert variant="error">
 304:           <p>{statusError}</p>
 305:           <button
 306:             type="button"
 307:             onClick={() => retry("connect")}
 308:             disabled={busy || !consent}
 309:             className={`${secondaryButton} mt-2`}
 310:           >
 311:             Tekrar dene
 312:           </button>
 313:         </Alert>
 314:       )}
 315: 
 316:       {canConnect && !statusError && (
 317:         <button
 318:           type="button"
 319:           onClick={handleConnect}
 320:           disabled={!consent || busy}
 321:           className={primaryButton}
 322:         >
 323:           Bağla
 324:         </button>
 325:       )}
 326: 
 327:       {groups !== undefined &&
 328:         (groups.ok ? (
 329:           // Salt-okunur durum canlı poll'dan (status) türer; durum ya da grup senkronu değişince
 330:           // seçici yeniden kurulur (seçim ve "not_connected" durumu sıfırlanır).
 331:           <GroupPicker
 332:             key={`${status.status}:${groups.groups_synced_at ?? ""}`}
 333:             initial={{ ...groups, status: status.status }}
 334:             onSave={setMyWaGroupSelection}
 335:           />
 336:         ) : (
 337:           <Alert variant="error">
 338:             <p>Grup listesi yüklenemedi, sayfayı yenileyin.</p>
 339:           </Alert>
 340:         ))}
 341: 
 342:       {confirmOpen && (
 343:         <div
 344:           role="dialog"
 345:           aria-modal="true"
 346:           aria-labelledby="wa-confirm-title"
 347:           className="space-y-3 rounded-md border border-mist-200 bg-fog-50 p-4"
 348:           onKeyDown={(e) => {
 349:             if (e.key === "Escape") setConfirmOpen(false);
 350:           }}
 351:         >
 352:           <h3 id="wa-confirm-title" className="font-semibold text-ink-900">
 353:             WhatsApp bağlantısı kaldırılsın mı?
 354:           </h3>
 355:           <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
 356:             Oturum kapatılır ve bağlantı bilgileriniz silinir. Yeniden bağlamak için QR taramanız gerekir.
 357:           </p>
 358:           <div className="flex gap-2">
 359:             <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmOpen(false)} className={secondaryButton}>
 360:               Vazgeç
 361:             </button>
 362:             <button type="button" onClick={handleDisconnect} disabled={busy} className={primaryButton}>
 363:               Evet, kaldır
 364:             </button>
 365:           </div>
 366:         </div>
 367:       )}
 368:     </section>
 369:   );
 370: }
===== web/src/app/agent/whatsapp/actions.ts =====
   1: "use server";
   2: 
   3: import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
   4: import {
   5:   setMyWaGroupSelection as setMyWaGroupSelectionRpc,
   6:   type WaSetGroupsResult,
   7: } from "@/lib/whatsapp/groups";
   8: import {
   9:   acceptWaConsent as acceptWaConsentRpc,
  10:   getMyWaStatus as getMyWaStatusRpc,
  11:   requestWaAction as requestWaActionRpc,
  12:   type WaConsentResult,
  13:   type WaActionResult,
  14:   type WaStatusResult,
  15: } from "@/lib/whatsapp/status";
  16: 
  17: // EML-106. Oturumlu istemci; agent_id hiçbir yerden alınmaz (AC-S1). Kimlik RPC içinde auth.uid().
  18: 
  19: const ACTIONS = ["connect", "disconnect"] as const;
  20: 
  21: export async function getMyWaStatus(): Promise<WaStatusResult> {
  22:   const supabase = await createClient();
  23:   return getMyWaStatusRpc(supabase);
  24: }
  25: 
  26: // EML-107 AC-12: "bağlantıyı kaldır" (disconnect) = bağlantıyı kes + verilerimi sil.
  27: // Önce bağlantı kesme isteği gider; ardından delete_my_wa_data çağrılır. Silme başarısızsa
  28: // (hata ya da throw) başarı bildirilmez: sessiz başarı yasak.
  29: // request_wa_action DB'de bağlantıyı 'disconnect' olarak işaretler. Bağlantı zaten kesikse
  30: // (not_connected) silme yine çağrılır: önceki denemede silme başarısız olduysa yeniden denemede
  31: // veri temizlenebilsin. Diğer hatalarda (rpc_failed, unauthorized vb.) silme çağrılmaz.
  32: export async function requestWaAction(action: string): Promise<WaActionResult> {
  33:   if (!(ACTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(action)) {
  34:     return { ok: false, error: "invalid_action" };
  35:   }
  36:   const supabase = await createClient();
  37:   const result = await requestWaActionRpc(supabase, action as (typeof ACTIONS)[number]);
  38:   if (action !== "disconnect") return result;
  39:   if (!result.ok && result.error !== "not_connected") return result;
  40: 
  41:   try {
  42:     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("delete_my_wa_data");
  43:     if (error) return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  44:     const r = (data !== null && typeof data === "object" ? data : {}) as { ok?: unknown };
  45:     if (r.ok !== true) return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  46:   } catch {
  47:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  48:   }
  49:   return result.ok ? result : { ok: true, status: "disconnected" };
  50: }
  51: 
  52: export async function acceptWaConsent(version: string): Promise<WaConsentResult> {
  53:   if (typeof version !== "string" || version.length === 0 || version.length > 32) {
  54:     return { ok: false, error: "invalid_version" };
  55:   }
  56:   const supabase = await createClient();
  57:   return acceptWaConsentRpc(supabase, version);
  58: }
  59: 
  60: // EML-107 AC-2: yalnız JID dizisi gider; üst sınır (20) ve JID doğrulaması RPC'de.
  61: export async function setMyWaGroupSelection(jids: string[]): Promise<WaSetGroupsResult> {
  62:   if (
  63:     !Array.isArray(jids) ||
  64:     !jids.every((jid) => typeof jid === "string" && jid.length > 0 && jid.length <= 200)
  65:   ) {
  66:     return { ok: false, error: "invalid_input" };
  67:   }
  68:   const supabase = await createClient();
  69:   return setMyWaGroupSelectionRpc(supabase, jids);
  70: }
===== web/src/app/agent/whatsapp/page.tsx =====
   1: import Link from "next/link";
   2: import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
   3: 
   4: import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
   5: import { getMyWaGroups } from "@/lib/whatsapp/groups";
   6: import { getMyWaStatus, type WaStatusView } from "@/lib/whatsapp/status";
   7: 
   8: import { WaPanel } from "./wa-panel";
   9: 
  10: // Dashboard ile aynı: oturum/çerez okunduğu için her istekte render edilir.
  11: export const instant = false;
  12: 
  13: // EML-106 / AC-11: bayrak yoksa sayfa yok gibi davranır (notFound).
  14: // Oturum yoksa /login. Bağlantı durumu ilk render'da sunucudan gelir; sonrası panel poll eder.
  15: export default async function WhatsAppPage() {
  16:   if (!process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WA_LINK_ENABLED) {
  17:     notFound();
  18:   }
  19: 
  20:   // createClient() cookies() okur: istek-zamanlı (dinamik) işaretleme getUser()'dan (Date.now)
  21:   // önce gerçekleşir. Bu yüzden ayrı connection() çağrısına gerek yoktur (admin layout'taki
  22:   // gibi next/server mock'u olmayan test ortamında da çalışır).
  23:   const supabase = await createClient();
  24:   const { data: auth } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  25:   if (!auth.user) {
  26:     redirect("/login");
  27:   }
  28: 
  29:   // Ban riski onayı kaydı (AC-1). Okunamazsa güvenli varsayılan: onaysız.
  30:   const { data: agent } = await supabase
  31:     .from("agents")
  32:     .select("wa_consent_accepted_at")
  33:     .eq("id", auth.user.id)
  34:     .maybeSingle();
  35:   const consentAccepted = Boolean(
  36:     (agent as { wa_consent_accepted_at: string | null } | null)?.wa_consent_accepted_at,
  37:   );
  38: 
  39:   const result = await getMyWaStatus(supabase);
  40:   const initialStatus: WaStatusView = result.ok
  41:     ? result
  42:     : { status: "error", error_code: null };
  43: 
  44:   return (
  45:     <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl bg-fog-50 p-4 md:p-8">
  46:       <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-ink-900">WhatsApp</h1>
  47:       <Link href="/agent/dashboard" className="mt-2 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline">
  48:         Panele dön
  49:       </Link>
  50:       <WaPanel
  51:         initialStatus={initialStatus}
  52:         consentAccepted={consentAccepted}
  53:         groups={await getMyWaGroups(supabase)}
  54:       />
  55:     </main>
  56:   );
  57: }
===== web/src/lib/whatsapp/groups.ts =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: 
   3: // EML-107 Davranış Sözleşmesi: get_my_wa_groups / set_my_wa_group_selection.
   4: // RPC sarmalayıcıları agent_id ALMAZ (AC-S1/AC-S2): kimlik her zaman auth.uid() ile.
   5: 
   6: export type WaGroup = { jid: string; name: string | null; selected: boolean };
   7: 
   8: export type WaGroupsOk = {
   9:   ok: true;
  10:   status: string;
  11:   groups: WaGroup[];
  12:   max: number;
  13:   groups_synced_at: string | null;
  14: };
  15: 
  16: export type WaGroupsResult = WaGroupsOk | { ok: false; error: string };
  17: 
  18: export type WaSetGroupsResult = { ok: true; selected: number } | { ok: false; error: string };
  19: 
  20: type RawRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
  21: 
  22: function asRecord(raw: unknown): RawRecord {
  23:   return raw !== null && typeof raw === "object" ? (raw as RawRecord) : {};
  24: }
  25: 
  26: function parseGroups(raw: unknown): WaGroup[] {
  27:   if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return [];
  28:   const groups: WaGroup[] = [];
  29:   for (const item of raw) {
  30:     const r = asRecord(item);
  31:     if (typeof r.jid !== "string") continue;
  32:     groups.push({
  33:       jid: r.jid,
  34:       name: typeof r.name === "string" ? r.name : null,
  35:       selected: r.selected === true,
  36:     });
  37:   }
  38:   return groups;
  39: }
  40: 
  41: /** Kendi WhatsApp gruplarım (get_my_wa_groups). */
  42: export async function getMyWaGroups(supabase: SupabaseClient): Promise<WaGroupsResult> {
  43:   try {
  44:     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_my_wa_groups");
  45:     if (error) return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  46:     const r = asRecord(data);
  47:     if (r.ok !== true) {
  48:       return { ok: false, error: typeof r.error === "string" ? r.error : "rpc_failed" };
  49:     }
  50:     return {
  51:       ok: true,
  52:       status: typeof r.status === "string" ? r.status : "none",
  53:       groups: parseGroups(r.groups),
  54:       max: typeof r.max === "number" ? r.max : 20,
  55:       groups_synced_at: typeof r.groups_synced_at === "string" ? r.groups_synced_at : null,
  56:     };
  57:   } catch {
  58:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  59:   }
  60: }
  61: 
  62: /** Okunacak grup seçimini tamamen değiştirir (set_my_wa_group_selection). */
  63: export async function setMyWaGroupSelection(
  64:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  65:   jids: string[],
  66: ): Promise<WaSetGroupsResult> {
  67:   try {
  68:     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("set_my_wa_group_selection", {
  69:       p_group_jids: jids,
  70:     });
  71:     if (error) return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  72:     const r = asRecord(data);
  73:     if (r.ok === true) {
  74:       return { ok: true, selected: typeof r.selected === "number" ? r.selected : 0 };
  75:     }
  76:     return { ok: false, error: typeof r.error === "string" ? r.error : "rpc_failed" };
  77:   } catch {
  78:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  79:   }
  80: }

  14: function dropEmptyFields(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
  15:   for (const element of Array.from(event.currentTarget.elements)) {
  16:     if (
  17:       (element instanceof HTMLInputElement || element instanceof HTMLSelectElement) &&
  18:       element.name &&
  19:       element.value === ""
  20:     ) {
  21:       element.disabled = true;
  22:     }
  23:   }
  24: }
