===== web/src/app/agent/dashboard/page.tsx =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: import Link from "next/link";
   3: import { connection } from "next/server";
   4: import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
   5: 
   6: import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
   7: import { parseFeedFilters, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/listings-feed/filters";
   8: import { fetchFeed, fetchFilterOptions } from "@/lib/listings-feed/query";
   9: import { getUnreadCount } from "@/lib/listings-feed/unread";
  10: import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
  11: 
  12: import { FeedFilters } from "./feed-filters";
  13: import { ListingCard } from "./listing-card";
  14: import { NotificationBell } from "./notification-bell";
  15: import { PushToggle } from "./push-toggle";
  16: 
  17: // Next.js 16 Cache Components: cookies() (Supabase session) her istekte
  18: // değişebilir, bu route segment'i prerender edilemez — statik build'i
  19: // engellemesin diye bloklu/dinamik render'a izin veriyoruz.
  20: export const instant = false;
  21: 
  22: type SubscriptionRow = {
  23:   status: string;
  24:   current_period_end: string | null;
  25: };
  26: 
  27: // Hata ekranındaki "Tekrar dene" — mevcut filtre/sayfa parametreleri korunur.
  28: function buildRetryHref(params: SearchParams): string {
  29:   const query = new URLSearchParams();
  30:   for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
  31:     if (Array.isArray(value)) {
  32:       for (const item of value) query.append(key, item);
  33:     } else if (value !== undefined) {
  34:       query.append(key, value);
  35:     }
  36:   }
  37:   const qs = query.toString();
  38:   return qs ? `/agent/dashboard?${qs}` : "/agent/dashboard";
  39: }
  40: 
  41: const BADGE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  42:   trialing: "Deneme sürümü",
  43:   active: "Aktif",
  44:   past_due: "Ödeme gecikmiş",
  45:   canceled: "İptal edildi",
  46: };
  47: 
  48: function badgeLabel(status: string | null): string {
  49:   if (!status) return "Aboneliğiniz yok";
  50:   return BADGE_LABELS[status] ?? `Abonelik: ${status}`;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: // Yalnız hasAccess sonrası çağrılır. Seçenekler, ilanlar ve sayaç sırayla
  54: // awaited edilir (aynı Supabase builder'ı üzerinde çakışmasın diye).
  55: async function loadListings(
  56:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  57:   params: SearchParams,
  58:   userId: string,
  59: ) {
  60:   const rawIl = typeof params.il === "string" ? params.il : null;
  61:   const options = await fetchFilterOptions(supabase, rawIl);
  62:   const { filters, hadInvalid } = parseFeedFilters(params, options);
  63:   const feed = await fetchFeed(supabase, filters);
  64: 
  65:   const { data: agent } = await supabase
  66:     .from("agents")
  67:     .select("listings_seen_at")
  68:     .eq("id", userId)
  69:     .maybeSingle();
  70:   const seenAt =
  71:     (agent as { listings_seen_at: string | null } | null)?.listings_seen_at ?? null;
  72:   const unread = await getUnreadCount(supabase, userId, seenAt);
  73: 
  74:   return { options, filters, hadInvalid, feed, unread };
  75: }
  76: 
  77: export default async function AgentDashboardPage({
  78:   searchParams,
  79: }: {
  80:   searchParams: Promise<SearchParams>;
  81: }) {
  82:   // Date.now (Supabase getUser ve aşağıdaki new Date() çağrıları) prerender
  83:   // sırasında çalışmasın diye ilk await'ten önce istek-zamanlı işaretleme yapılır.
  84:   await connection();
  85:   const params = await searchParams;
  86:   const supabase = await createClient();
  87:   const { data: auth } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  88:   if (!auth.user) {
  89:     redirect("/login");
  90:   }
  91: 
  92:   // Önce açık (trialing/active/past_due) abonelik, yoksa en son satır (rozet için).
  93:   let sub: SubscriptionRow | null = null;
  94:   let subError = false;
  95: 
  96:   const open = await supabase
  97:     .from("subscriptions")
  98:     .select("status, current_period_end")
  99:     .eq("agent_id", auth.user.id)
 100:     .in("status", ["trialing", "active", "past_due"])
 101:     .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
 102:     .limit(1)
 103:     .maybeSingle();
 104: 
 105:   if (open.error) {
 106:     subError = true;
 107:   } else {
 108:     sub = (open.data as SubscriptionRow | null) ?? null;
 109:   }
 110: 
 111:   if (!subError && !sub) {
 112:     const latest = await supabase
 113:       .from("subscriptions")
 114:       .select("status, current_period_end")
 115:       .eq("agent_id", auth.user.id)
 116:       .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
 117:       .limit(1)
 118:       .maybeSingle();
 119: 
 120:     if (latest.error) {
 121:       subError = true;
 122:     } else {
 123:       sub = (latest.data as SubscriptionRow | null) ?? null;
 124:     }
 125:   }
 126: 
 127:   // AC-6: abonelik hiç yoksa ve deneme daha önce açılmadıysa start_trial() bir kez dener.
 128:   let trialFailed = false;
 129:   if (!subError && sub === null) {
 130:     const { data: agent, error: agentError } = await supabase
 131:       .from("agents")
 132:       .select("trial_started_at")
 133:       .eq("id", auth.user.id)
 134:       .maybeSingle();
 135:     const trialStartedAt =
 136:       (agent as { trial_started_at: string | null } | null)?.trial_started_at ?? null;
 137: 
 138:     if (agentError) {
 139:       trialFailed = true;
 140:       console.error("trial_create_failed", { agent_id: auth.user.id, reason: "agent_read_failed" });
 141:     } else if (trialStartedAt === null) {
 142:       const { data: started, error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc("start_trial");
 143:       if (rpcError) {
 144:         trialFailed = true;
 145:         console.error("trial_create_failed", {
 146:           agent_id: auth.user.id,
 147:           error_code: rpcError.code ?? "unknown",
 148:         });
 149:       } else if (started === true) {
 150:         const created = await supabase
 151:           .from("subscriptions")
 152:           .select("status, current_period_end")
 153:           .eq("agent_id", auth.user.id)
 154:           .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
 155:           .limit(1)
 156:           .maybeSingle();
 157:         if (created.error) {
 158:           subError = true;
 159:         } else {
 160:           sub = (created.data as SubscriptionRow | null) ?? null;
 161:         }
 162:       } else {
 163:         trialFailed = true;
 164:         console.error("trial_create_failed", { agent_id: auth.user.id, reason: "no_row" });
 165:       }
 166:     }
 167:   }
 168: 
 169:   const badge = subError
 170:     ? "Abonelik durumu yüklenemedi"
 171:     : badgeLabel(sub?.status ?? null);
 172: 
 173:   // Ön kontrol (UX): satır var ve dönem süresi dolmamış. Durum kuralı (active /
 174:   // trialing / past_due) feed_access() içinde tek yerde tutulur; sunucu
 175:   // aboneliksiz derse feedNoSubscription ile erişim ekranına düşülür.
 176:   const hasAccess =
 177:     !subError &&
 178:     sub !== null &&
 179:     sub.current_period_end !== null &&
 180:     new Date(sub.current_period_end) > new Date();
 181: 
 182:   const listings = hasAccess
 183:     ? await loadListings(supabase, params, auth.user.id)
 184:     : null;
 185:   const filters = listings?.filters;
 186:   const isFiltered =
 187:     filters !== undefined &&
 188:     (filters.grup !== null ||
 189:       filters.fiyatMin !== null ||
 190:       filters.fiyatMax !== null ||
 191:       filters.oda !== null ||
 192:       filters.il !== null ||
 193:       filters.ilce !== null ||
 194:       filters.kapsam !== null);
 195:   const now = new Date();
 196:   // Sunucu aboneliksiz derse (reason) erişim ekranı gösterilir, liste gizlenir.
 197:   const feedNoSubscription =
 198:     listings?.feed.ok === true && listings.feed.reason === "no_subscription";
 199:   const showNoAccess = !hasAccess || feedNoSubscription;
 200:   // Şifre yenileme sonrası yönlendirme işareti (/sifre-yenile).
 201:   const passwordUpdated = params.password_updated === "1";
 202: 
 203:   return (
 204:     <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl bg-fog-50 p-4 md:p-8">
 205:       <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
 206:         <div>
 207:           <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-ink-900">Emlakçı Paneli</h1>
 208:           <Link href="/agent/videos" className="mt-2 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline">
 209:             Video siparişlerim
 210:           </Link>
 211:           {process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WA_LINK_ENABLED && (
 212:             <Link href="/agent/whatsapp" className="mt-2 ml-4 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline">
 213:               WhatsApp&apos;ını bağla
 214:             </Link>
 215:           )}
 216:         </div>
 217:         {listings && !feedNoSubscription && (
 218:           <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
 219:             <PushToggle />
 220:             <NotificationBell initialCount={listings.unread ?? 0} />
 221:           </div>
 222:         )}
 223:       </div>
 224:       <p className="mt-2 inline-block rounded border border-mist-200 px-2 py-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
 225:         {badge}
 226:       </p>
 227: 
 228:       {passwordUpdated && (
 229:         <div className="mt-6">
 230:           <Alert variant="success">Şifreniz güncellendi.</Alert>
 231:         </div>
 232:       )}
 233: 
 234:       {trialFailed && (
 235:         <>
 236:           <div className="mt-6">
 237:             <Alert variant="error">Denemeniz başlatılamadı, destekle iletişime geçin</Alert>
 238:           </div>
 239:           <Link href="/pricing" className="mt-2 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline">
 240:             Fiyatları gör
 241:           </Link>
 242:         </>
 243:       )}
 244: 
 245:       {showNoAccess && (
 246:         <div className="mt-6 text-slate-500">
 247:           <p>İlanları görmek için aktif abonelik gerekir.</p>
 248:           <Link href="/account/subscription" className="mt-2 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline">
 249:             Abone olun
 250:           </Link>
 251:         </div>
 252:       )}
 253: 
 254:       {listings && !feedNoSubscription && (
 255:         <>
 256:           <FeedFilters options={listings.options} filters={listings.filters} />
 257: 
 258:           {listings.hadInvalid && (
 259:             <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
 260:               Bazı filtreler geçersizdi, uygulanmadı
 261:             </p>
 262:           )}
 263: 
 264:           {!listings.feed.ok && (
 265:             <div className="mt-6">
 266:               <Alert variant="error">İlanlar yüklenemedi.</Alert>
 267:               <Link
 268:                 href={buildRetryHref(params)}
 269:                 className="mt-2 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline"
 270:               >
 271:                 Tekrar dene
 272:               </Link>
 273:             </div>
 274:           )}
 275: 
 276:           {listings.feed.ok && listings.feed.limit !== null && (
 277:             <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
 278:               <span>{`Bugün ${listings.feed.openToday}/${listings.feed.limit} ilan açık`}</span>
 279:               {(listings.feed.lockedCount === null || listings.feed.lockedCount > 0) && (
 280:                 <Link href="/pricing" className="text-terracotta-700 underline">
 281:                   Planı yükselt
 282:                 </Link>
 283:               )}
 284:             </div>
 285:           )}
 286: 
 287:           {listings.feed.ok && listings.feed.items.length === 0 && (
 288:             <div className="mt-6 text-slate-500">
 289:               <p>
 290:                 {isFiltered
 291:                   ? "Filtreye uyan ilan yok."
 292:                   : "Şu an gösterilecek ilan yok."}
 293:               </p>
 294:               {isFiltered && (
 295:                 <Link href="/agent/dashboard" className="mt-2 inline-block text-terracotta-700 underline">
 296:                   Filtreleri temizle
 297:                 </Link>
 298:               )}
 299:             </div>
 300:           )}
 301: 
 302:           {listings.feed.ok && listings.feed.items.length > 0 && (
 303:             <ul className="mt-6 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
 304:               {listings.feed.items.map((listing) => (
 305:                 <ListingCard key={listing.id} listing={listing} now={now} />
 306:               ))}
 307:             </ul>
 308:           )}
 309:         </>
 310:       )}
 311:     </main>
 312:   );
 313: }
===== web/src/lib/listings-feed/filters.ts =====
   1: export const ROOM_OPTIONS = ["1+0", "1+1", "2+1", "3+1", "4+1", "5+"] as const;
   2: 
   3: export type FeedFilterOptions = {
   4:   cities: string[];
   5:   districts: string[];
   6:   groups: string[];
   7: };
   8: 
   9: export type ParsedFilters = {
  10:   grup: string | null;
  11:   fiyatMin: number | null;
  12:   fiyatMax: number | null;
  13:   oda: string | null;
  14:   il: string | null;
  15:   ilce: string | null;
  16:   /** EML-107 AC-8: "mine" yalnız kendi gruplarımdaki kişisel ilanları getirir; null = tümü. */
  17:   kapsam: "mine" | null;
  18:   sayfa: number;
  19: };
  20: 
  21: export type SearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
  22: 
  23: const MAX_PRICE = 1_000_000_000_000;
  24: const MAX_TEXT_LENGTH = 100;
  25: const MAX_PAGE = 1000;
  26: 
  27: type Picked<T> = { value: T | null; invalid: boolean };
  28: 
  29: // Parametre yoksa geçersiz sayılmaz; varsa ama parse edilemiyorsa geçersizdir.
  30: function pick<T>(
  31:   raw: string | string[] | undefined,
  32:   parse: (value: string) => T | null,
  33: ): Picked<T> {
  34:   if (raw === undefined) return { value: null, invalid: false };
  35:   const value = typeof raw === "string" ? parse(raw) : null;
  36:   return { value, invalid: value === null };
  37: }
  38: 
  39: function parsePrice(value: string): number | null {
  40:   if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) return null;
  41:   const amount = Number(value);
  42:   return amount <= MAX_PRICE ? amount : null;
  43: }
  44: 
  45: function parseScope(value: string): "mine" | null {
  46:   return value === "mine" ? "mine" : null;
  47: }
  48: 
  49: function parseRoom(value: string): string | null {
  50:   return (ROOM_OPTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(value) ? value : null;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: function allowOnly(allowed: string[]) {
  54:   return (value: string): string | null =>
  55:     value.length <= MAX_TEXT_LENGTH && allowed.includes(value) ? value : null;
  56: }
  57: 
  58: function parsePage(raw: string | string[] | undefined): number {
  59:   if (typeof raw !== "string") return 1;
  60:   const page = Number.parseInt(raw, 10);
  61:   if (!Number.isFinite(page)) return 1;
  62:   return Math.min(MAX_PAGE, Math.max(1, page));
  63: }
  64: 
  65: export function parseFeedFilters(
  66:   params: SearchParams,
  67:   options: FeedFilterOptions,
  68: ): { filters: ParsedFilters; hadInvalid: boolean } {
  69:   const min = pick(params.fiyat_min, parsePrice);
  70:   const max = pick(params.fiyat_max, parsePrice);
  71:   const oda = pick(params.oda, parseRoom);
  72:   const il = pick(params.il, allowOnly(options.cities));
  73:   const ilce = pick(params.ilce, allowOnly(options.districts));
  74:   const grup = pick(params.grup, allowOnly(options.groups));
  75:   const kapsam = pick(params.kapsam, parseScope);
  76: 
  77:   let fiyatMin = min.value;
  78:   let fiyatMax = max.value;
  79:   if (fiyatMin !== null && fiyatMax !== null && fiyatMin > fiyatMax) {
  80:     [fiyatMin, fiyatMax] = [fiyatMax, fiyatMin];
  81:   }
  82: 
  83:   const hadInvalid = [min, max, oda, il, ilce, grup, kapsam].some((p) => p.invalid);
  84: 
  85:   return {
  86:     filters: {
  87:       grup: grup.value,
  88:       fiyatMin,
  89:       fiyatMax,
  90:       oda: oda.value,
  91:       il: il.value,
  92:       ilce: ilce.value,
  93:       kapsam: kapsam.value,
  94:       sayfa: parsePage(params.sayfa),
  95:     },
  96:     hadInvalid,
  97:   };
  98: }
===== web/src/lib/listings-feed/query.ts =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: 
   3: import type { FeedFilterOptions, ParsedFilters } from "./filters";
   4: 
   5: export const QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;
   6: 
   7: // Açık satır: sunucu tüm ilan alanlarını döner (EML-98 AC-1).
   8: export type FeedRow = {
   9:   id: string;
  10:   title: string;
  11:   price: number | null;
  12:   currency: string;
  13:   rooms: string | null;
  14:   area_m2: number | null;
  15:   city: string | null;
  16:   district: string | null;
  17:   source_group_name: string | null;
  18:   created_at: string;
  19:   /** EML-107 AC-8: dolu ise kişisel ilan (kendi grubum) — rozet için. */
  20:   owner_agent_id?: string | null;
  21: };
  22: 
  23: export type OpenFeedRow = FeedRow & { locked: false };
  24: 
  25: // Kilitli satır: yalnız bu 5 anahtar (AC-4 / AC-S2). Başka alan gelmez.
  26: export type LockedFeedRow = {
  27:   id: string;
  28:   city: string | null;
  29:   district: string | null;
  30:   created_at: string;
  31:   locked: true;
  32: };
  33: 
  34: export type FeedItem = OpenFeedRow | LockedFeedRow;
  35: 
  36: export type FeedResult =
  37:   | {
  38:       ok: true;
  39:       items: FeedItem[];
  40:       lockedCount: number | null;
  41:       limit: number | null;
  42:       openToday: number;
  43:       reason?: string;
  44:     }
  45:   | { ok: false; code: string };
  46: 
  47: type FeedRpcBody = {
  48:   items?: FeedItem[];
  49:   locked_count?: number;
  50:   limit?: number | null;
  51:   open_today?: number;
  52:   reason?: string;
  53: };
  54: 
  55: export async function fetchFeed(
  56:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  57:   filters: ParsedFilters,
  58: ): Promise<FeedResult> {
  59:   try {
  60:     const { data, error } = await supabase
  61:       .rpc("feed_listings", {
  62:         p_filters: {
  63:           il: filters.il,
  64:           ilce: filters.ilce,
  65:           grup: filters.grup,
  66:           fiyatMin: filters.fiyatMin,
  67:           fiyatMax: filters.fiyatMax,
  68:           oda: filters.oda,
  69:           // scope yalnız "mine" iken gider; varsayılan çağrı şekli değişmez.
  70:           ...(filters.kapsam === "mine" ? { scope: "mine" } : {}),
  71:         },
  72:         p_page: filters.sayfa,
  73:       })
  74:       .abortSignal(AbortSignal.timeout(QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS));
  75: 
  76:     if (error) {
  77:       console.error("[dashboard] feed rpc failed", { code: error.code });
  78:       return { ok: false, code: error.code ?? "unknown" };
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     const body = (data ?? {}) as FeedRpcBody;
  82:     const result: FeedResult = {
  83:       ok: true,
  84:       items: body.items ?? [],
  85:       lockedCount: body.locked_count ?? null,
  86:       limit: body.limit ?? null,
  87:       openToday: body.open_today ?? 0,
  88:     };
  89:     if (body.reason !== undefined) result.reason = body.reason;
  90:     return result;
  91:   } catch (err) {
  92:     const code = err instanceof Error ? err.name : "unknown";
  93:     console.error("[dashboard] feed rpc failed", { code });
  94:     return { ok: false, code };
  95:   }
  96: }
  97: 
  98: export async function fetchFilterOptions(
  99:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
 100:   il: string | null,
 101: ): Promise<FeedFilterOptions> {
 102:   const empty: FeedFilterOptions = { cities: [], districts: [], groups: [] };
 103: 
 104:   try {
 105:     const { data, error } = await supabase
 106:       .rpc("feed_filter_options", { p_il: il ?? null })
 107:       .abortSignal(AbortSignal.timeout(QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS));
 108: 
 109:     if (error) {
 110:       console.error("[dashboard] feed options rpc failed", { code: error.code });
 111:       return empty;
 112:     }
 113: 
 114:     const body = (data ?? {}) as Partial<FeedFilterOptions>;
 115:     return {
 116:       cities: body.cities ?? [],
 117:       districts: body.districts ?? [],
 118:       groups: body.groups ?? [],
 119:     };
 120:   } catch (err) {
 121:     const code = err instanceof Error ? err.name : "unknown";
 122:     console.error("[dashboard] feed options rpc failed", { code });
 123:     return empty;
 124:   }
 125: }
===== web/src/app/admin/listings/threshold-form.tsx =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import { type FormEvent, useState } from "react";
   4: 
   5: import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
   6: 
   7: // EML-107 AC-7 (eşik UI) ve AC-S5. Eşik app_settings('personal_auto_approve_threshold'); 0-1 dışı reddedilir.
   8: // Kaydetme onSave prop'undan (server action → admin_set_threshold RPC) gelir.
   9: 
  10: const RANGE_ERROR = "0 ile 1 arasında bir değer girin";
  11: const SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  12:   forbidden: "Yetkiniz yok",
  13:   invalid_value: RANGE_ERROR,
  14: };
  15: const DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR = "Kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.";
  16: 
  17: export type ThresholdSaveResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };
  18: 
  19: type Notice = { kind: "ok" | "error"; text: string } | null;
  20: 
  21: // Boş, sayı olmayan, sonsuz ve 0-1 dışı değerler geçersiz.
  22: function parseThreshold(input: string): number | null {
  23:   if (input.trim() === "") return null;
  24:   const n = Number(input);
  25:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 1 ? n : null;
  26: }
  27: 
  28: export function ThresholdForm({
  29:   value,
  30:   onSave,
  31: }: {
  32:   value: number;
  33:   onSave: (value: number) => Promise<ThresholdSaveResult>;
  34: }) {
  35:   const [input, setInput] = useState(String(value));
  36:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  37:   const [notice, setNotice] = useState<Notice>(null);
  38: 
  39:   async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
  40:     event.preventDefault();
  41:     const parsed = parseThreshold(input);
  42:     if (parsed === null) {
  43:       setNotice({ kind: "error", text: RANGE_ERROR });
  44:       return;
  45:     }
  46:     setBusy(true);
  47:     setNotice(null);
  48:     const res = await onSave(parsed);
  49:     setBusy(false);
  50:     if (res.ok) {
  51:       setNotice({ kind: "ok", text: "Kaydedildi" });
  52:       return;
  53:     }
  54:     setNotice({ kind: "error", text: SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES[res.error] ?? DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR });
  55:   }
  56: 
  57:   return (
  58:     <form noValidate onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-4 space-y-3">
  59:       <label className="block text-sm text-zinc-700">
  60:         Otomatik onay eşiği (0-1)
  61:         <input
  62:           type="number"
  63:           min={0}
  64:           max={1}
  65:           step="0.01"
  66:           inputMode="decimal"
  67:           value={input}
  68:           onChange={(event) => setInput(event.target.value)}
  69:           className="mt-1 block w-full max-w-xs rounded border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
  70:         />
  71:       </label>
  72:       <button
  73:         type="submit"
  74:         disabled={busy}
  75:         className="rounded bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-zinc-700 disabled:opacity-50"
  76:       >
  77:         Kaydet
  78:       </button>
  79:       {notice && (
  80:         <Alert variant={notice.kind === "ok" ? "success" : "error"}>
  81:           <p>{notice.text}</p>
  82:         </Alert>
  83:       )}
  84:     </form>
  85:   );
  86: }
===== web/src/app/admin/listings/page.tsx =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
   3: 
   4: import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/requireAdmin";
   5: 
   6: import { approveListing, rejectListing, setPersonalThreshold } from "./actions";
   7: import { ThresholdForm } from "./threshold-form";
   8: 
   9: const THRESHOLD_KEY = "personal_auto_approve_threshold";
  10: const DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.7;
  11: 
  12: // EML-107 AC-7: mevcut eşik. app_settings doğrudan okunmaz (RLS kapalı); admin_get_threshold RPC'si
  13: // kontrolü yapıp yalnız eşiği döndürür. Hata/forbidden → varsayılan gösterilir ve bu ayrıca belirtilir.
  14: async function readThreshold(
  15:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  16: ): Promise<{ value: number; failed: boolean }> {
  17:   try {
  18:     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_get_threshold");
  19:     if (error) return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
  20:     const r = (data ?? null) as { ok?: unknown; value?: unknown } | null;
  21:     if (r === null || r.ok !== true) return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
  22:     // ok:true ama değer sayı değilse şema/veri bozulmuş demektir: varsayılan gösterilir ve bu belirtilir.
  23:     return typeof r.value === "number" && Number.isFinite(r.value)
  24:       ? { value: r.value, failed: false }
  25:       : { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
  26:   } catch {
  27:     return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
  28:   }
  29: }
  30: 
  31: type ParsedListing = {
  32:   title?: string | null;
  33:   price?: number | null;
  34:   currency?: string | null;
  35:   rooms?: string | null;
  36:   area_m2?: number | null;
  37:   city?: string | null;
  38:   district?: string | null;
  39:   neighborhood?: string | null;
  40:   description?: string | null;
  41: };
  42: 
  43: type RawListingRow = {
  44:   id: string;
  45:   source_group: string | null;
  46:   raw_text: string;
  47:   parsed: ParsedListing;
  48:   quality_score: number | null;
  49:   created_at: string;
  50:   /** EML-107 AC-6: dolu ise kişisel ilan (emlakçının kendi grubu). */
  51:   owner_agent_id: string | null;
  52:   /** owner_agent_id FK'si üzerinden açık embed (reviewed_by ile iki ilişki var, PGRST201 önlenir). */
  53:   owner: { company_name: string | null } | null;
  54: };
  55: 
  56: export default async function AdminListingsPage() {
  57:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  58:   if (!auth.ok) {
  59:     redirect(auth.reason === "no-session" ? "/login" : "/agent/dashboard");
  60:   }
  61:   const { supabase } = auth;
  62: 
  63:   const { data: rawListings, error } = await supabase
  64:     .from("raw_listings")
  65:     .select(
  66:       "id, source_group, raw_text, parsed, quality_score, created_at, owner_agent_id, owner:agents!raw_listings_owner_agent_id_fkey(company_name)",
  67:     )
  68:     .eq("status", "pending")
  69:     .order("created_at", { ascending: true })
  70:     .returns<RawListingRow[]>();
  71: 
  72:   if (error) {
  73:     console.error("raw_listings sorgusu başarısız", error);
  74:     return (
  75:       <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl p-8">
  76:         <p className="text-red-600">İlanlar yüklenemedi, lütfen tekrar deneyin.</p>
  77:       </main>
  78:     );
  79:   }
  80: 
  81:   const threshold = await readThreshold(supabase);
  82: 
  83:   return (
  84:     <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl p-8">
  85:       <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">İlan Onay Paneli</h1>
  86: 
  87:       <section className="mt-6 rounded border p-4">
  88:         <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">Kişisel ilan otomatik onay eşiği</h2>
  89:         <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">
  90:           Eşiğin altındaki kişisel ilanlar bu kuyruğa düşer. Varsayılan 0.70.
  91:         </p>
  92:         {threshold.failed && (
  93:           <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">
  94:             Mevcut eşik okunamadı; 0.70 varsayılan gösteriliyor.
  95:           </p>
  96:         )}
  97:         <ThresholdForm value={threshold.value} onSave={setPersonalThreshold} />
  98:       </section>
  99: 
 100:       <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
 101:         {rawListings?.length ?? 0} incelenmemiş ilan.
 102:       </p>
 103: 
 104:       <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
 105:         {rawListings?.map((item) => (
 106:           <article key={item.id} className="rounded border p-4">
 107:             <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-500">
 108:               <span>{item.source_group ?? "bilinmeyen grup"}</span>
 109:               <span>skor: {item.quality_score ?? "—"}</span>
 110:             </div>
 111:             {item.owner_agent_id && (
 112:               <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700">
 113:                 {`Kişisel — ${item.owner?.company_name ?? "bilinmeyen emlakçı"}`}
 114:               </p>
 115:             )}
 116: 
 117:             <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm">{item.raw_text}</p>
 118: 
 119:             <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 text-sm text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
 120:               <dt>Başlık</dt>
 121:               <dd>{item.parsed.title ?? "—"}</dd>
 122:               <dt>Fiyat</dt>
 123:               <dd>
 124:                 {item.parsed.price ?? "—"} {item.parsed.currency ?? ""}
 125:               </dd>
 126:               <dt>Oda</dt>
 127:               <dd>{item.parsed.rooms ?? "—"}</dd>
 128:               <dt>m²</dt>
 129:               <dd>{item.parsed.area_m2 ?? "—"}</dd>
 130:               <dt>Konum</dt>
 131:               <dd>
 132:                 {[item.parsed.neighborhood, item.parsed.district, item.parsed.city]
 133:                   .filter(Boolean)
 134:                   .join(", ") || "—"}
 135:               </dd>
 136:             </dl>
 137: 
 138:             <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
 139:               <form action={approveListing}>
 140:                 <input type="hidden" name="raw_listing_id" value={item.id} />
 141:                 <button
 142:                   type="submit"
 143:                   className="rounded bg-green-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-green-700"
 144:                 >
 145:                   Onayla
 146:                 </button>
 147:               </form>
 148:               <form action={rejectListing}>
 149:                 <input type="hidden" name="raw_listing_id" value={item.id} />
 150:                 <button
 151:                   type="submit"
 152:                   className="rounded bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-red-700"
 153:                 >
 154:                   Reddet
 155:                 </button>
 156:               </form>
 157:             </div>
 158:           </article>
 159:         ))}
 160: 
 161:         {rawListings?.length === 0 && (
 162:           <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">İncelenmemiş ilan yok.</p>
 163:         )}
 164:       </div>
 165:     </main>
 166:   );
 167: }
===== web/src/app/admin/listings/actions.ts =====
   1: "use server";
   2: 
   3: import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
   4: import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
   5: 
   6: import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/requireAdmin";
   7: 
   8: export async function approveListing(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
   9:   const rawListingId = String(formData.get("raw_listing_id") ?? "");
  10:   if (!rawListingId) return;
  11: 
  12:   // Uygulama seviyesi admin kontrolü — RPC'den ÖNCE. RLS/SECURITY INVOKER
  13:   // ikinci katman olarak kalır (defense-in-depth).
  14:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  15:   if (!auth.ok) {
  16:     if (auth.reason === "no-session") {
  17:       redirect("/login");
  18:     }
  19:     throw new Error("Bu işlem için yetkiniz yok");
  20:   }
  21:   const { supabase } = auth;
  22: 
  23:   const { error } = await supabase.rpc("approve_raw_listing", {
  24:     p_raw_listing_id: rawListingId,
  25:   });
  26: 
  27:   if (error) {
  28:     console.error("İlan onaylanamadı", error);
  29:     throw new Error("İlan onaylanamadı, lütfen tekrar deneyin");
  30:   }
  31: 
  32:   revalidatePath("/admin/listings");
  33: }
  34: 
  35: // EML-107 AC-7 / AC-S5: eşik yalnız admin RPC'siyle değişir. Yetki kontrolü RPC'den önce;
  36: // RPC de kendi içinde admin doğrular (defense-in-depth).
  37: export async function setPersonalThreshold(
  38:   value: number,
  39: ): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }> {
  40:   if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > 1) {
  41:     return { ok: false, error: "invalid_value" };
  42:   }
  43: 
  44:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  45:   if (!auth.ok) {
  46:     if (auth.reason === "no-session") {
  47:       redirect("/login");
  48:     }
  49:     return { ok: false, error: "forbidden" };
  50:   }
  51:   const { supabase } = auth;
  52: 
  53:   const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_set_threshold", { p_threshold: value });
  54:   if (error) {
  55:     console.error("eşik güncellenemedi", error);
  56:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  57:   }
  58:   const result = (data ?? {}) as { ok?: boolean; error?: string };
  59:   if (result.ok === true) {
  60:     revalidatePath("/admin/listings");
  61:     return { ok: true };
  62:   }
  63:   return { ok: false, error: typeof result.error === "string" ? result.error : "rpc_failed" };
  64: }
  65: 
  66: export async function rejectListing(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  67:   const rawListingId = String(formData.get("raw_listing_id") ?? "");
  68:   if (!rawListingId) return;
  69: 
  70:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  71:   if (!auth.ok) {
  72:     if (auth.reason === "no-session") {
  73:       redirect("/login");
  74:     }
  75:     throw new Error("Bu işlem için yetkiniz yok");
  76:   }
  77:   const { supabase } = auth;
  78: 
  79:   const { error } = await supabase.rpc("reject_raw_listing", {
  80:     p_raw_listing_id: rawListingId,
  81:   });
  82: 
  83:   if (error) {
  84:     console.error("İlan reddedilemedi", error);
  85:     throw new Error("İlan reddedilemedi, lütfen tekrar deneyin");
  86:   }
  87: 
  88:   revalidatePath("/admin/listings");
  89: }

 127:         client = get_client()
 128:         key_manager = get_default_manager()
 129: 
 130:         if agent_id:
 131:             # KVKK: seçim önbelleği (sidecar, 60 sn) eski olabilir; metin parse'a (Gemini) gitmeden
 132:             # önce grubun bu emlakçı için HÂLÂ seçili olduğu DB'den doğrulanır.
 133:             try:
 134:                 selected = is_group_selected(client, agent_id, group)
 135:             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - pesimist: doğrulanamazsa parse etme, içerik loglanmaz
 136:                 logger.warning("Seçim doğrulanamadı, mesaj parse edilmedi (içerik loglanmaz)")
 137:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_selection_check_error", agent_id)
 138:             if not selected:
 139:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_unselected", agent_id)
 140:             try:
 141:                 parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
 142:             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - içerik loglanmaz, yalnız sayaç
 143:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_parse_error", agent_id)
 144:             if parsed is None:
 145:                 return _count_personal(client, "personal_not_listing", agent_id)
 146:             return _handle_personal(client, agent_id, payload, message_id, text, parsed)
 147: 
 148:         parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
 149: 
 150:         if parsed is None:
 151:             logger.info("Mesaj ilan değil veya parse edilemedi, atlandı: %s", message_id)
 152:             return None
 153: 
 154:         fingerprint = compute_fingerprint(parsed)
 155:         if fingerprint_exists(client, fingerprint):
 156:             logger.info("Mükerrer ilan (fingerprint eşleşti), atlandı: %s", message_id)
 157:             return None
 158: 
 159:         score, issues = score_listing(parsed)
 160:         record = {
 161:             "source": "whatsapp",
 162:             "source_group": group,
 163:             "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
 164:             "source_message_id": message_id or None,
 165:             "raw_text": text,
 166:             "parsed": parsed,
 167:             "fingerprint": fingerprint,
 168:             "quality_score": score,
 169:             "status": "pending",
 170:             "owner_agent_id": None,
 171:         }
 172:         _inserted_row, inserted = insert_raw_listing(client, record)
 173:         if inserted:
 174:             logger.info(
 175:                 "raw_listings'e yazıldı: %s (skor=%.2f, auto_approve_uygun=%s, sorunlar=%s)",
 176:                 message_id,
 177:                 score,
 178:                 is_safe_to_auto_approve(score, float(os.getenv("QUALITY_GATE_THRESHOLD", "0.5"))),
 179:                 issues,
 180:             )
 181:         else:
 182:             logger.info(
 183:                 "Mükerrer ilan (fingerprint çakışması, DB seviyesinde atlandı): %s",
 184:                 message_id,
 185:             )
 186:     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - worker thread hatayı yutmasın, logla ve devam et
 187:         logger.exception("Mesaj işlenirken hata: %s", message_id)
 188:     finally:
 189:         with _active_ids_lock:
 190:             _active_ids.discard(message_id)
 191:     return None
 192:

 194: def _background_worker_loop(executor: ThreadPoolExecutor) -> None:
 195:     while True:
 196:         payload = message_queue.get()
 197:         executor.submit(handle_message, payload)

 200: class WebhookHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
 201:     def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - http.server API ismi
 202:         if self.path != "/baileys-webhook":
 203:             self.send_response(404)
 204:             self.end_headers()
 205:             return
 206: 
 207:         secret = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")
 208:         auth_header = self.headers.get("Authorization", "")
 209:         token = auth_header[len("Bearer "):] if auth_header.startswith("Bearer ") else ""
 210:         if not secret or not hmac.compare_digest(token, secret):
 211:             body = json.dumps({"error": "unauthorized"}).encode("utf-8")
 212:             self.send_response(401)
 213:             self.send_header("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer")
 214:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 215:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
 216:             self.end_headers()
 217:             self.wfile.write(body)
 218:             return
 219: 
 220:         length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
 221:         body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
 222:         try:
 223:             payload = json.loads(body or b"{}")
 224:         except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError):
 225:             # Body içeriği loglanmaz (PII riski).
 226:             logger.warning("Geçersiz webhook body (JSON/UTF-8 çözülemedi), 400 dönüldü.")
 227:             response = json.dumps({"error": "invalid_body"}).encode("utf-8")
 228:             self.send_response(400)
 229:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 230:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 231:             self.end_headers()
 232:             self.wfile.write(response)
 233:             return
 234: 
 235:         if not payload.get("text"):
 236:             # Metinsiz mesaj kuyruğa alınmaz; 200 ile "işlendi" denir (WhatsApp tekrar göndermesin).
 237:             response = b"{}"
 238:             self.send_response(200)
 239:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 240:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 241:             self.end_headers()
 242:             self.wfile.write(response)
 243:             return
 244: 
 245:         message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
 246:         with _active_ids_lock:
 247:             if message_id and message_id in _active_ids:
 248:                 self.send_response(200)
 249:                 self.end_headers()
 250:                 return
 251:             if message_id:
 252:                 _active_ids.add(message_id)
 253: 
 254:         message_queue.put(payload)
 255: 
 256:         self.send_response(200)
 257:         self.end_headers()
 258: 
 259:     def log_message(self, format: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: A002 - http.server imzası
 260:         logger.debug("%s - %s", self.address_string(), format % args)

  25: export function GroupPicker({ initial, onSave }: GroupPickerProps) {
  26:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(
  27:     () => new Set(initial.groups.filter((g) => g.selected).map((g) => g.jid)),
  28:   );
  29:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  30:   const [notice, setNotice] = useState<Notice>(null);
  31:   // Kaydetmede not_connected dönerse, sayfa yenilenene kadar salt-okunur kalır.
  32:   const [notConnected, setNotConnected] = useState(false);
  33: 
  34:   const readonly = initial.status !== "connected" || notConnected;
  35: 
  36:   if (initial.status === "none") {
  37:     return (
  38:       <section aria-labelledby="wa-groups-title" className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
  39:         <h2 id="wa-groups-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
  40:           Grup seçimi
  41:         </h2>
  42:         <p className="text-sm text-ink-900">{"Önce WhatsApp'ını bağla"}</p>
  43:       </section>
  44:     );
  45:   }
  46: 
  47:   const awaitingSync = initial.groups.length === 0 && initial.groups_synced_at === null;
  48: 
  49:   function toggle(jid: string) {
  50:     setNotice(null);
  51:     setSelected((prev) => {
  52:       const next = new Set(prev);
  53:       if (next.has(jid)) next.delete(jid);
  54:       else if (next.size < initial.max) next.add(jid);
  55:       return next;
  56:     });
  57:   }
  58: 
  59:   async function handleSave() {
  60:     const jids = initial.groups.filter((g) => selected.has(g.jid)).map((g) => g.jid);
  61:     setBusy(true);
  62:     setNotice(null);
  63:     const res = await onSave(jids);
  64:     setBusy(false);
  65:     if (res.ok) {
  66:       setNotice(
  67:         res.selected === 0
  68:           ? { kind: "ok", text: "Hiçbir grup okunmuyor" }
  69:           : { kind: "ok", text: "Kaydedildi" },
  70:       );
  71:       return;
  72:     }
  73:     if (res.error === "not_connected") {
  74:       setNotConnected(true);
  75:       return;
  76:     }
  77:     setNotice({ kind: "error", text: SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES[res.error] ?? DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR });
  78:   }
  79: 
  80:   const primaryButton =
  81:     "rounded-md bg-terracotta-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:opacity-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-ink-900 disabled:opacity-50";
  82: 
  83:   return (
  84:     <section aria-labelledby="wa-groups-title" className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
  85:       <h2 id="wa-groups-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
  86:         Grup seçimi
  87:       </h2>
  88: 
  89:       {readonly && (
  90:         <Alert variant="error">
  91:           <p>
  92:             WhatsApp bağlantınız aktif değil; grup seçimi salt okunur. <strong>Yeniden bağlan</strong>
  93:           </p>
  94:         </Alert>
  95:       )}
  96: 
  97:       {awaitingSync ? (
  98:         <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Gruplar yükleniyor…</p>
  99:       ) : (
 100:         <>
 101:           <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{`${selected.size}/${initial.max}`}</p>
 102:           <ul className="space-y-2">
 103:             {initial.groups.map((group) => {
 104:               const checked = selected.has(group.jid);
 105:               const full = !checked && selected.size >= initial.max;
 106:               return (
 107:                 <li key={group.jid}>
 108:                   <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-ink-900">
 109:                     <input
 110:                       type="checkbox"
 111:                       className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0"
 112:                       checked={checked}
 113:                       disabled={readonly || busy || full}
 114:                       onChange={() => toggle(group.jid)}
 115:                     />
 116:                     <span className="min-w-0 break-words">{group.name ?? "(adsız grup)"}</span>
 117:                   </label>
 118:                 </li>
 119:               );
 120:             })}
 121:           </ul>
 122: 
 123:           {initial.groups.length > 0 && (
 124:             <button
 125:               type="button"
 126:               onClick={handleSave}
 127:               disabled={readonly || busy}
 128:               className={primaryButton}
 129:             >
 130:               Kaydet
 131:             </button>
 132:           )}
 133:         </>
 134:       )}
 135: 
 136:       {notice && (
 137:         <Alert variant={notice.kind === "ok" ? "success" : "error"}>
 138:           <p>{notice.text}</p>
 139:         </Alert>
 140:       )}
 141:     </section>
 142:   );
 143: }

  53: // Panel yalnız yerel "kaldırılıyor" ara durumunu ekler; diğerleri sunucu şekli.
  54: type ViewStatus = WaStatusView | { status: "disconnecting" };
  55: 
  56: type RetryKind = "consent" | "connect" | "disconnect";
  57: type PanelError = { message: string; retry?: RetryKind } | null;
  58: 
  59: export type WaPanelProps = {
  60:   initialStatus: WaStatusView;
  61:   consentAccepted: boolean;
  62:   /** EML-107: grup listesi (sunucu, get_my_wa_groups). Yoksa grup bölümü gösterilmez. */

  64: };
  65: 
  66: /** Hata/durum mesajı gereken durumlar; "Bağla" yerine "Tekrar dene" gösterilir. */
  67: function statusMessage(status: ViewStatus): string | null {
  68:   switch (status.status) {
  69:     case "logged_out":
  70:       return LOGGED_OUT_MESSAGE;
  71:     case "disconnected": {
  72:       const known = status.error_code ? DISCONNECTED_MESSAGES[status.error_code] : undefined;
  73:       return known ?? "Bağlantı kesildi, tekrar bağlayabilirsiniz.";
  74:     }
  75:     case "error": {
  76:       const known = status.error_code ? STATUS_ERROR_MESSAGES[status.error_code] : undefined;
  77:       return known ?? DEFAULT_ERROR;
  78:     }
  79:     default:
  80:       return null;
  81:   }
  82: }
  83: 
  84: function actionErrorMessage(code: string): string {
  85:   return ACTION_ERROR_MESSAGES[code] ?? DEFAULT_ERROR;
  86: }
  87: 
  88: // Kaldırma sırasında sidecar henüz işlemediyse "connected" görünür; yalnız sonuç durumları kabul edilir.
  89: const DISCONNECT_TERMINAL = new Set(["none", "disconnected", "error", "logged_out"]);
  90: 
  91: function applyPoll(prev: ViewStatus, res: WaStatusResult, timedOut: boolean): ViewStatus {
  92:   if (!res.ok) return { status: "error", error_code: null };
  93:   if (prev.status === "disconnecting") {
  94:     if (timedOut) return { status: "error", error_code: "sidecar_timeout" };
  95:     return DISCONNECT_TERMINAL.has(res.status) ? res : prev;
  96:   }
  97:   return res;
  98: }
  99: 
 100: export function WaPanel({ initialStatus, consentAccepted, groups }: WaPanelProps) {
 101:   const [status, setStatus] = useState<ViewStatus>(initialStatus);
 102:   const [consent, setConsent] = useState(consentAccepted);
 103:   const [ticked, setTicked] = useState(false);
 104:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
 105:   const [error, setError] = useState<PanelError>(null);
 106:   const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
 107:   const [qrImage, setQrImage] = useState<{ value: string; svg: string } | null>(null);
 108:   const pollCountRef = useRef(0);
 109:   const router = useRouter();
 110:   const lastStatusRef = useRef<string>(initialStatus.status);
 111: 
 112:   const isPolling =
 113:     status.status === "pending" || status.status === "qr" || status.status === "disconnecting";
 114: 
 115:   // Bağlantı durumu değiştiğinde (ör. connected) sunucu bileşeni yeniden çalışır ve grup listesi
 116:   // tazelenir; mount'taki ilk durum değişimi sayılmaz.
 117:   useEffect(() => {
 118:     if (lastStatusRef.current === status.status) return;
 119:     lastStatusRef.current = status.status;
 120:     router.refresh();
 121:   }, [status.status, router]);
 122: 
 123:   // 2 sn poll: yalnız bekleme/QR/kaldırma sürerken.
 124:   useEffect(() => {
 125:     if (!isPolling) return;
 126:     const id = setInterval(async () => {
 127:       pollCountRef.current += 1;
 128:       const timedOut = pollCountRef.current > DISCONNECT_MAX_POLLS;
 129:       const res = await getMyWaStatus();
 130:       setStatus((prev) => applyPoll(prev, res, timedOut));
 131:     }, POLL_MS);
 132:     return () => clearInterval(id);
 133:   }, [isPolling]);
 134: 
 135:   const qrValue = status.status === "qr" ? status.qr : null;
 136: 
 137:   // QR SVG: qrcode kütüphanesi (yalnız kendi üretimi; kullanıcı girdisi içermez).
 138:   useEffect(() => {
 139:     if (qrValue === null) return;
 140:     let cancelled = false;
 141:     QRCode.toString(qrValue, { type: "svg", margin: 1, width: 240, errorCorrectionLevel: "M" })
 142:       .then((svg) => {
 143:         if (!cancelled) setQrImage({ value: qrValue, svg });
 144:       })
 145:       .catch(() => {
 146:         if (!cancelled) setError({ message: "QR kodu gösterilemedi, tekrar deneyin.", retry: "connect" });
 147:       });
 148:     return () => {
 149:       cancelled = true;
 150:     };
 151:   }, [qrValue]);
 152: 
 153:   async function handleAccept() {
 154:     if (!ticked) return;
 155:     setBusy(true);
 156:     setError(null);
 157:     const res = await acceptWaConsent(WA_CONSENT_VERSION);
 158:     setBusy(false);
 159:     if (res.ok) {
 160:       setConsent(true);
 161:       setTicked(false);
 162:       return;
 163:     }
 164:     setError({ message: "Onay kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.", retry: "consent" });
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   async function handleConnect() {
 168:     setBusy(true);
 169:     setError(null);
 170:     const res = await requestWaAction("connect");
 171:     setBusy(false);
 172:     if (!res.ok) {
 173:       if (res.error === "consent_required") {
 174:         // Sunucu onayı görmedi: onay kutusu yeniden gösterilir, Tekrar dene sunulmaz.
 175:         setConsent(false);
 176:         setTicked(false);
 177:         setError({ message: "Devam etmek için riski onaylamanız gerekir." });
 178:         return;
 179:       }
 180:       setError({ message: actionErrorMessage(res.error), retry: "connect" });
